### [1] [Quantifying Confidence in Assurance 2.0 Arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00034)
*Robin Bloomfield,John Rushby*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单、系统且健全的评估保障案例论证中概率置信度的新方法，通过利用结构化论证中声明分解的方式，根据子声明之间的（不）独立性和多样性程度提供不同方法，使用Frechet边界等基本概率构造，无需概率论背景即可应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置信度是安全与保障案例的核心要素，决策所需的置信度与论证实际提供的置信度之间存在重要关系。现有方法存在缺陷（Graydon和Holloway已发现反例），需要一种更简单、系统且健全的置信度评估方法。

Method: 该方法利用结构化论证中声明分解的特性，根据子声明间的（不）独立性和多样性程度以及消除损害父声明置信度关切的方式，提供差异化方法。核心采用Frechet边界等 elementary probabilistic constructions，并针对保障论证场景进行专门解释，使不具备概率分析背景的用户也能使用。

Result: 该方法能够规避Graydon和Holloway针对其他方法所指出的反例问题，证明了方法的健全性。

Conclusion: 该方法被推荐作为保障2.0（Assurance 2.0）论证评估的补充工具。保障2.0的主要评估标准仍为逻辑不可废止性和辩证审查，但概率评估可用于评估不同风险等级的成本/置信度权衡，以及结构化论证中置信度的整体平衡。

Abstract: Confidence is central to safety and assurance cases: how much confidence a decision requires and how much the argument actually provides are both important questions. We present a new method for assessing probabilistic confidence in assurance case arguments that is simple, systematic and sound.
  It exploits the ways claims are decomposed in a structured argument and provides different approaches according to the different degrees of (in)dependence and diversity among subclaims and the way they eliminate concerns that undermine confidence in their parent claims. The method uses only elementary probabilistic constructions that are well-known in other contexts (e.g., Frechet bounds) but we interpret and apply them in a manner that is specifically focused on assurance arguments and requires no background in probabilistic analysis.
  We show that the method is not susceptible to the counterexamples that Graydon and Holloway exhibit for other approaches to confidence and we recommend it as an additional tool in evaluation of Assurance 2.0 arguments. The primary evaluation criteria for Assurance 2.0 remain logical indefeasibility and dialectical examination, but probabilistic assessment can be useful in evaluating cost/confidence tradeoffs for different risk levels, and the overall balance of confidence across a structured argument.

</details>


### [2] [Large Language Models for Analyzing Enterprise Architecture Debt in Unstructured Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00046)
*Christin Pagels,Simon Hacks,Rob Henk Bemthuis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出使用大型语言模型（LLM）从非结构化架构文档中自动检测企业架构气味（EA Smells），以识别企业架构债务（EA Debt）。通过设计科学研究方法，开发并评估了一个基于LLM的原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EA Smells检测主要依赖人工方式或仅分析结构化工件，导致大量非结构化文档（如流程描述、战略文档等）未被充分利用，无法及时发现因设计决策不当和组件错位导致的架构债务问题。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，设计并开发了LLM原型系统。系统接收非结构化文档输入，通过微调的检测模型识别EA Smells并输出结果。研究使用合成但真实的业务文档进行案例研究，并将自定义GPT模型作为基准进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明LLM能够从非结构化文本中检测多种预定义的EA Smells。基准GPT模型在精确率和处理速度上表现更优，而本地部署的微调模型则在数据保护方面具有优势。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的EA Smell检测技术具有良好的应用前景，可有效集成到企业架构治理实践中，帮助组织更全面地识别和量化架构债务。

Abstract: Enterprise Architecture Debt (EA Debt) arises from suboptimal design decisions and misaligned components that can degrade an organization's IT landscape over time. Early indicators, Enterprise Architecture Smells (EA Smells), are currently mainly detected manually or only from structured artifacts, leaving much unstructured documentation under-analyzed. This study proposes an approach using a large language model (LLM) to identify and quantify EA Debt in unstructured architectural documentation. Following a design science research approach, we design and evaluate an LLM-based prototype for automated EA Smell detection. The artifact ingests unstructured documents (e.g., process descriptions, strategy papers), applies fine-tuned detection models, and outputs identified smells. We evaluate the prototype through a case study using synthetic yet realistic business documents, benchmarking against a custom GPT-based model. Results show that LLMs can detect multiple predefined EA Smells in unstructured text, with the benchmark model achieving higher precision and processing speed, and the fine-tuned on-premise model offering data protection advantages. The findings highlight opportunities for integrating LLM-based smell detection into EA governance practice.

</details>


### [3] [The Energy Footprint of LLM-Based Environmental Analysis: LLMs and Domain Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00053)
*Alicia Bao,Jiamian He,Angel Hsu,Diego Manya,Ji,Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于LLM的气候分析聊天机器人（ChatNetZero和ChatNDC）与通用GPT-4o-mini模型的推理时能耗，对比了RAG系统与直接使用通用LLM的能耗差异，发现更"智能化"的RAG系统设计会显著增加能耗，但不一定带来相应的输出质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在气候与环境研究等特定领域的应用增长，理解其能源足迹变得至关重要。检索增强生成（RAG）系统在气候领域的广泛采用引发了一个关键问题：领域特定RAG工作流程的能耗与传统直接使用通用LLM相比如何？现有研究主要关注独立模型调用或粗略的token估算，对已部署应用工作流程的能源影响理解不足。

Method: 将每个RAG工作流程分解为检索、生成和幻觉检查三个组件，在不同时间段和地理访问位置下，使用实际用户查询来估算能耗。

Result: 领域特定RAG系统的能耗高度依赖于其设计。更智能化的管道会显著增加推理时能耗，特别是在用于额外准确性或验证检查时，尽管可能不会带来与能耗增加成正比的响应质量提升。

Conclusion: RAG系统的设计对LLM产品的能源足迹和输出质量都有显著影响，但需要进一步在不同模型、环境和提示结构下进行更稳健的验证。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in domain-specific applications, including climate change and environmental research, understanding their energy footprint has become an important concern. The growing adoption of retrieval-augmented (RAG) systems for climate-domain specific analysis raises a key question: how does the energy consumption of domain-specific RAG workflows compare with that of direct generic LLM usage? Prior research has focused on standalone model calls or coarse token-based estimates, while leaving the energy implications of deployed application workflows insufficiently understood. In this paper, we assess the inference-time energy consumption of two LLM-based climate analysis chatbots (ChatNetZero and ChatNDC) compared to the generic GPT-4o-mini model. We estimate energy use under actual user queries by decomposing each workflow into retrieval, generation, and hallucination-checking components. We also test across different times of day and geographic access locations. Our results show that the energy consumption of domain-specific RAG systems depends strongly on their design. More agentic pipelines substantially increase inference-time energy use, particularly when used for additional accuracy or verification checks, although they may not yield proportional gains in response quality. While more research is needed to further test these initial findings more robustly across models, environments and prompting structures, this study provides a new understanding on how the design of domain-specific LLM products affects both the energy footprint and quality of output.

</details>


### [4] [Terminal Agents Suffice for Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00073)
*Patrice Bechard,Orlando Marquez Ayala,Emily Chen,Jordan Skelton,Sagar Davasam,Srinivas Sunkara,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨企业任务自动化中不同智能体架构的优劣，提出配备终端和文件系统的简单编程式智能体（低层级终端智能体）能够匹配或超越复杂的多层级智能体架构（如MCP工具增强型智能体、GUI网页智能体），认为基础模型结合简单程序化接口足以满足企业自动化需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前复杂企业任务自动化主要依赖两种方案：(1) 基于模型上下文协议(MCP)等抽象的工具增强型智能体；(2) 通过图形界面操作的网页智能体。然而，这些系统存在高昂成本和运营开销，研究者质疑复杂架构的必要性，寻求更高效的替代方案。

Method: 作者团队构建了仅配备终端和文件系统的低层级编程式智能体，通过直接调用平台API完成各类企业任务，并在多个真实系统中进行了对比评估实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种低层级终端智能体在企业任务执行中能够匹配甚至超越更复杂的智能体架构。

Conclusion: 简单程序化接口结合强大的基础模型足以支撑实际企业自动化场景，无需依赖复杂的多层级智能体抽象架构。

Abstract: There has been growing interest in building agents that can interact with digital platforms to execute meaningful enterprise tasks autonomously. Among the approaches explored are tool-augmented agents built on abstractions such as Model Context Protocol (MCP) and web agents that operate through graphical interfaces. Yet, it remains unclear whether such complex agentic systems are necessary given their cost and operational overhead. We argue that a coding agent equipped only with a terminal and a filesystem can solve many enterprise tasks more effectively by interacting directly with platform APIs. We evaluate this hypothesis across diverse real-world systems and show that these low-level terminal agents match or outperform more complex agent architectures. Our findings suggest that simple programmatic interfaces, combined with strong foundation models, are sufficient for practical enterprise automation.

</details>


### [5] [An Empirical Study on How Architectural Topology Affects Microservice Performance and Energy Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00080)
*Irena Ristova,Vincenzo Stoico*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究使用μBench框架，在5、10、20服务规模下测试了6种典型微服务拓扑（顺序扇出、并行扇出、链式、分层、概率、网格）的能源效率，发现拓扑结构对能源效率有显著影响，其中Mesh拓扑性能最差，而分层、链式和扇出设计能更好地平衡性能和能耗，随着系统规模扩大，指标趋于收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构虽具可扩展性和弹性，但云原生环境下的能源效率问题日益突出。尽管现有研究关注微服务的分解和部署策略，但拓扑结构（即服务间的结构排列和交互模式）对能源效率的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 使用μBench框架构建6种标准拓扑结构（顺序扇出、并行扇出、链式、分层、概率、网格），每种拓扑在5、10、20服务规模下进行测试，在相同工作负载下测量吞吐量、响应时间、能源消耗、CPU利用率和故障率。

Result: 拓扑结构影响微服务能源效率；随系统规模增大，能源消耗增长，Mesh和链式拓扑增长最陡；Mesh拓扑整体性能最差（低吞吐量、长响应时间、高故障率）；分层、链式、扇出设计能更好平衡性能和能耗；系统扩展时指标趋于收敛，概率和并行扇出在CPU受限负载下最节能。

Conclusion: 研究结果为绿色微服务架构设计提供指导，并为未来工作负载和部署影响研究提供基准。研究确认了拓扑设计选择对能源效率的重要性。

Abstract: Microservice architectures form the backbone of modern software systems for their scalability, resilience, and maintainability, but their rise in cloud-native environments raises energy efficiency concerns. While prior research addresses microservice decomposition and placement, the impact of topology, the structural arrangement and interaction pattern among services, on energy efficiency remains largely underexplored. This study quantifies the impact of topologies on energy efficiency and performance across six canonical ones (Sequential Fan-Out, Parallel Fan-Out, Chain, Hierarchical, Probabilistic, Mesh), each instantiated at 5-, 10-, and 20-service scales using the $μ\text{Bench}$ framework. We measure throughput, response time, energy usage, CPU utilization, and failure rates under an identical workload. The results indicate that topology influences the energy efficiency of microservices under the studied conditions. As system size increases, energy consumption grows, with the steepest rise observed in dense Mesh and Chain topologies. Mesh topologies perform worst overall, with low throughput, long response times, and high failure rates. Hierarchical, Chain, and Fan-Out designs balance performance and energy use better. As systems scale, metrics converge, with Probabilistic and Parallel Fan-Out emerging as the most energy-efficient under CPU-bound loads. These results guide greener microservice architecture design and serve as a baseline for future research on workload and deployment impacts.

</details>


### [6] [From Domain Understanding to Design Readiness: a playbook for GenAI-supported learning in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00120)
*Rafal Wlodarski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Software engineering courses often require rapid upskilling in supporting knowledge areas such as domain understanding and modeling methods. We report an experience from a two-week milestone in a master's course where 29 students used a customized ChatGPT (GPT-3.5) tutor grounded in a curated course knowledge base to learn cryptocurrency-finance basics and Domain-Driven Design (DDD). We logged all interactions and evaluated a 34.5% random sample of prompt-answer pairs (60/~174) with a five-dimension rubric (accuracy, relevance, pedagogical value, cognitive load, supportiveness), and we collected pre/post self-efficacy. Responses were consistently accurate and relevant in this setting: accuracy averaged 98.9% with no factual errors and only 2/60 minor inaccuracies, and relevance averaged 92.2%. Pedagogical value was high (89.4%) with generally appropriate cognitive load (82.78%), but supportiveness was low (37.78%). Students reported large pre-post self-efficacy gains for genAI-assisted domain learning and DDD application. From these observations we distill seventeen concrete teaching practices spanning prompt/configuration and course/workflow design (e.g., setting expected granularity, constraining verbosity, curating guardrail examples, adding small credit with a simple quality rubric). Within this single-course context, results suggest that genAI-supported learning can complement instruction in domain understanding and modeling tasks, while leaving room to improve tone and follow-up structure.

</details>


### [7] [A Study on the Impact of Fault localization Granularity for Repository-Scale Code Repair Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00167)
*Joseph Townsend,Chandresh Pravin,Kwun Ho Ngan,Matthieu Parizy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码修复中故障定位粒度（函数级、行级、文件级）对自动程序修复效果的影响。通过修改Agentless框架获取真实定位数据，在SWE-Bench-Mini数据集上证明函数级粒度效果最佳，但最佳粒度可能因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未在完美定位假设下系统比较不同定位粒度对仓库级代码修复的影响，缺乏对这两个阶段关系的深入理解。

Method: 修改Agentless框架的定位阶段，获取真实定位数据作为上下文注入修复提示词；在SWE-Bench-Mini数据集上测试函数级、行级、文件级三种粒度。

Result: 函数级粒度在总体修复率上优于行级和文件级；但深入分析表明，最佳粒度可能取决于具体任务。

Conclusion: 定位粒度对修复效果有显著影响，理想粒度可能与任务特性相关；本研究为探索定位-修复关系提供了概念验证框架。

Abstract: Automatic program repair can be a challenging task, especially when resolving complex issues at a repository-level, which often involves issue reproduction, fault localization, code repair, testing and validation. Issues of this scale can be commonly found in popular GitHub repositories or datasets that are derived from them.
  Some repository-level approaches separate localization and repair into distinct phases. Where this is the case, the fault localization approaches vary in terms of the granularity of localization. Where the impact of granularity is explored to some degree for smaller datasets, not all isolate this issue from the separate question of localization accuracy by testing code repair under the assumption of perfect fault localization. To the best of the authors' knowledge, no repository-scale studies have explicitly investigated granularity under this assumption, nor conducted a systematic empirical comparison of granularity levels in isolation.
  We propose a framework for performing such tests by modifying the localization phase of the Agentless framework to retrieve ground-truth localization data and include this as context in the prompt fed to the repair phase. We show that under this configuration and as a generalization over the SWE-Bench-Mini dataset, function-level granularity yields the highest repair rate against line-level and file-level. However, a deeper dive suggests that the ideal granularity may in fact be task dependent.
  This study is not intended to improve on the state-of-the-art, nor do we intend for results to be compared against any complete agentic frameworks. Rather, we present a proof of concept for investigating how fault localization may impact automatic code repair in repository-scale scenarios. We present preliminary findings to this end and encourage further research into this relationship between the two phases.

</details>


### [8] [Making Sense of AI Agents Hype: Adoption, Architectures, and Takeaways from Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00189)
*Ruoyu Su,Matteo Esposito,Roberta Capuano,Rafiullah Omar,June Sallou,Henry Muccini,Davide Taibi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对138场 practitioners 会议演讲的系统性回顾，研究现实工业实践中 agentic systems 的设计方式，旨在理解企业如何采用基于 agent 的架构、识别常见的架构策略与模式，以及分析应用领域和技术实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 技术的发展，agentic systems 在工业界的应用日益广泛，但现有研究多聚焦于学术层面，缺乏对实际工业实践的系统性理解。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为从业者提供实用的指导。

Method: 系统性文献综述方法，对138场 practitioners 会议演讲（而非学术论文）进行编码分析。这些演讲来源于真实的工业实践案例，能够反映当前企业采用 agent 技术的真实情况。

Result: 研究从三个维度展开分析：(1) 企业 adoption of agent-based architectures 的现状；(2) 常见的 architectural strategies and patterns；(3) 应用领域分布及使用的技术栈。

Conclusion: 研究为理解真实世界中 LLM-driven agentic systems 的设计与实现提供了实践层面的洞察，有助于指导后续的系统设计与技术选型决策。

Abstract: To support practitioners in understanding how agentic systems are designed in real-world industrial practice, we present a review of practitioner conference talks on AI agents. We analyzed 138 recorded talks to examine how companies adopt agent-based architectures (Objective 1), identify recurring architectural strategies and patterns (Objective 2), and analyze application domains and technologies used to implement and operate LLM-driven agentic systems (Objective 3).

</details>


### [9] [Risk-Aware Batch Testing for Performance Regression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00222)
*Ali Sayedsalehi,Peter C. Rigby,Gregory Mierzwinski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险感知框架，将机器学习预测的代码提交性能回归风险与自适应批处理策略相结合，用于优化大规模CI系统中的性能回归测试。通过Mozilla Firefox进行案例研究，最优配置RAPB-la实现了32.4%的测试执行减少、3.8%的平均反馈时间降低、26.2%的最大问题定位时间减少，并估算每年节省约49.1万美元基础设施成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模CI系统中全量性能测试成本过高，现有方法存在局限：(1)性能回归预测模型难以真正集成到CI决策流程；(2)传统批处理策略忽略提交级别的异质性。因此需要一种统一的风险感知框架来平衡测试资源消耗与回归检测效率。

Method: 1) 使用Mozilla Firefox构建生产级数据集，包含人工确认的性能回归及其在Autoland中的时间序对应；2) 微调ModernBERT、CodeBERT和LLaMA-3.1变体预测提交级性能回归风险（最高达0.694 ROC-AUC）；3) 基于风险分数设计风险感知批处理策略族，包括Risk-Aged Priority Batching (RAPB)和Risk-Adaptive Stream Batching；4) 通过真实CI模拟进行评估。

Result: 最优配置RAPB-la（线性聚合的风险老化优先批处理）在数千个Firefox历史提交上实现了Pareto改进：测试执行减少32.4%，平均反馈时间降低3.8%，平均问题定位时间持平，最大问题定位时间减少26.2%，估算年节省基础设施成本约49.1万美元。

Conclusion: 风险感知批处理测试可有效降低CI资源消耗同时提升问题诊断时效性。该研究提供了完整复现包（含数据集、微调流程和批处理算法实现），为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Performance regression testing is essential in large-scale continuous-integration (CI) systems, yet executing full performance suites for every commit is prohibitively expensive. Prior work on performance regression prediction and batch testing has shown independent benefits, but each faces practical limitations: predictive models are rarely integrated into CI decision-making, and conventional batching strategies ignore commit-level heterogeneity.
  We unify these strands by introducing a risk-aware framework that integrates machine-learned commit risk with adaptive batching. Using Mozilla Firefox as a case study, we construct a production-derived dataset of human-confirmed regressions aligned chronologically with Autoland, and fine-tune ModernBERT, CodeBERT, and LLaMA-3.1 variants to estimate commit-level performance regression risk, achieving up to 0.694 ROC-AUC with CodeBERT. The risk scores drive a family of risk-aware batching strategies, including Risk-Aged Priority Batching and Risk-Adaptive Stream Batching, evaluated through realistic CI simulations.
  Across thousands of historical Firefox commits, our best overall configuration, Risk-Aged Priority Batching with linear aggregation (RAPB-la), yields a Pareto improvement over Mozilla's production-inspired baseline. RAPB-la reduces total test executions by 32.4%, decreases mean feedback time by 3.8%, maintains mean time-to-culprit at approximately the baseline level, reduces maximum time-to-culprit by 26.2%, and corresponds to an estimated annual infrastructure cost savings of approximately $491K under our cost model. These results demonstrate that risk-aware batch testing can reduce CI resource consumption while improving diagnostic timeliness. To support reproducibility and future research, we release a complete replication package containing all datasets, fine-tuning pipelines, and implementations of our batching algorithms.

</details>


### [10] [VeriAct: Beyond Verifiability -- Agentic Synthesis of Correct and Complete Formal Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00280)
*Md Rakib Hossain Misu,Iris Ma,Cristina V. Lopes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型自动生成JML形式化规格说明的质量问题进行了深入研究。发现现有方法即使通过提示优化提升了验证通过率，仍存在大量规格说明不正确或不完整的问题。为此提出了Spec-Harness评估框架和VeriAct迭代修复框架，后者通过闭环反馈机制生成真正正确且完整的规格说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规格说明对软件可靠性至关重要，但自动合成高质量规格说明仍具挑战性。现有基于LLM的方法报告了较高的JML规格说明验证通过率，但通过验证器是否等同于规格说明的正确性和完整性尚不明确。这一问题暴露了形式化规格说明自动生成领域的核心质量评估缺口。

Method: 研究采用多层递进的方法：(1) 综合对比经典方法与基于提示的方法在JML规格说明合成上的表现；(2) 通过结构化验证反馈优化提示词，评估优化效果；(3) 提出Spec-Harness评估框架，通过符号验证衡量规格说明的正确性和完整性；(4) 提出VeriAct验证引导的智能体框架，采用LLM驱动规划、代码执行、验证和Spec-Harness反馈的闭环迭代机制。

Result: 实验揭示了两个关键发现：首先，提示优化虽能提升验证器通过率，但存在明显的性能上限；其次，Spec-Harness评估发现相当比例通过验证的规格说明（包括优化后的）实际上是不正确或不完整的，对输入输出存在过度或不足约束。更重要的是，VeriAct在两个基准数据集上显著优于基于提示的方法和提示优化基线方法。

Conclusion: VeriAct框架能够生成不仅通过验证器检验、而且在语义上正确且完整的规格说明，有效突破了提示优化的性能天花板。该工作为形式化规格说明的自动化合成提供了新的评估标准和迭代优化范式。

Abstract: Formal specifications play a central role in ensuring software reliability and correctness. However, automatically synthesizing high-quality formal specifications remains a challenging task, often requiring domain expertise. Recent work has applied large language models to generate specifications in Java Modeling Language (JML), reporting high verification pass rates. But does passing a verifier mean that the specification is actually correct and complete? In this work, we first conduct a comprehensive evaluation comparing classical and prompt-based approaches for automated JML specification synthesis. We then investigate whether prompt optimization can push synthesis quality further by evolving prompts through structured verification feedback. While optimization improves verifier pass rates, we find a clear performance ceiling. More critically, we propose Spec-Harness, an evaluation framework that measures specification correctness and completeness through symbolic verification, revealing that a large fraction of verifier-accepted specifications, including optimized ones, are in fact incorrect or incomplete, over- or under-constraining both inputs and outputs in ways invisible to the verifier. To push beyond this ceiling, we propose VeriAct, a verification-guided agentic framework that iteratively synthesizes and repairs specifications through a closed loop of LLM-driven planning, code execution, verification, and Spec-Harness feedback. Our experiments on two benchmark datasets show that VeriAct outperforms both prompt-based and prompt-optimized baselines, producing specifications that are not only verifiable but also correct and complete.

</details>


### [11] [When is Generated Code Difficult to Comprehend? Assessing AI Agent Python Code Proficiency in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00299)
*Nanthit Temkulkiat,Chaiyong Ragkhitwetsagul,Morakot Choetkiertikul,Ruksit Rojpaisarnkit,Raula Gaikovina Kula*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究AI编程智能体生成的Python代码的语言熟练度和复杂性，发现90%以上的代码属于A1-A2基础级别，但特定功能添加和bug修复任务可能需要高级熟练度才能审查和维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体快速普及导致开发者角色从代码编写者转变为代码审查者，但目前对AI生成代码的语言熟练度和复杂性缺乏系统研究，这影响开发者评估维护AI代码所需技能水平的能力。

Method: 使用AIDev数据集，挖掘591个包含5,027个Python文件的pull request，运用pycefr静态分析工具将Python代码构造映射到六个熟练度等级（A1-C2），分析三个不同AI智能体生成的代码。

Result: 超过90%的代码构造属于A1和A2基础级别，仅不到1%达到C2精通级别；AI智能体与人类pull request的熟练度分布大体相似；高级熟练度代码主要来自功能添加和bug修复任务。

Conclusion: AI生成代码总体上对具备基础Python技能的开发者友好，但特定任务生成的复杂代码结构仍需高级熟练度才能有效审查和维护。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding agents is fundamentally shifting software developers' roles from code authors to code reviewers. While developers spend a significant portion of their time reading and comprehending code, the linguistic proficiency and complexity of the Python code generated by these agents remain largely unexplored. This study investigates the code proficiency of AI agents to determine the skill level required for developers to maintain their code. Leveraging the AIDev dataset, we mined 591 pull requests containing 5,027 Python files generated by three distinct AI agents and employed pycefr, a static analysis tool that maps Python constructs to six proficiency levels, ranging from A1 (Basic) to C2 (Mastery), to analyze the code. Our results reveal that: AI agents predominantly generate Basic-level code, with over 90% of constructs falling into the A1 and A2 categories, and less than 1% classified as Mastery (C2); AI agents' and humans' pull requests share a broadly similar proficiency profile; High-proficiency code by AI agents are from feature addition and bug fixing tasks. These findings suggest that while AI-generated code is generally accessible to developers with basic Python skills, specific tasks may require advanced proficiency to review and maintain complex, agent-generated constructs.

</details>


### [12] [Programming by Chat: A Large-Scale Behavioral Analysis of 11,579 Real-World AI-Assisted IDE Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00436)
*Ningzhi Tang,Chaoran Chen,Zihan Fang,Gelei Xu,Maria Dhakal,Yiyu Shi,Collin McMillan,Yu Huang,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次大规模实证研究IDE原生环境下对话式编程，分析了近7.5万条开发者消息，揭示了AI辅助编程的三个核心转变：渐进式规格化、认知工作转移、以及主动协作管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于小规模受控实验或通用聊天机器人，缺乏对真实代码库感知型IDE工作流程的深入理解。研究者希望填补这一空白，首次大规模研究真实场景中的对话式编程行为。

Method: 收集1,300个开源仓库中作为常规开发流程一部分而提交的聊天记录，包含74,998条开发者消息、11,579个对话会话，涉及899名开发者和Cursor、GitHub Copilot两款工具。数据来源于公开代码库，反映"野外"真实行为。

Result: 发现AI辅助编程呈现三个组织特征：1) 渐进式规格化——开发者迭代式优化输出，而非预先完整定义任务；2) 认知工作转移——开发者将诊断、理解、验证等任务委托给AI，而非直接参与代码和输出；3) 主动协作管理——开发者将计划外部化到持久工件，通过上下文注入和行为约束来协商AI自主性。

Conclusion: 研究为AI辅助开发提供了基础性实证洞察，揭示了开发者与AI协作的新模式，对未来编程环境的设计具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: IDE-integrated AI coding assistants, which operate conversationally within developers' working codebases with access to project context and multi-file editing, are rapidly reshaping software development. However, empirical investigation of this shift remains limited: existing studies largely rely on small-scale, controlled settings or analyze general-purpose chatbots rather than codebase-aware IDE workflows. We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale study of real-world conversational programming in IDE-native settings, analyzing 74,998 developer messages from 11,579 chat sessions across 1,300 repositories and 899 developers using Cursor and GitHub Copilot. These chats were committed to public repositories as part of routine development, capturing in-the-wild behavior. Our findings reveal three shifts in how programming work is organized: conversational programming operates as progressive specification, with developers iteratively refining outputs rather than specifying complete tasks upfront; developers redistribute cognitive work to AI, delegating diagnosis, comprehension, and validation rather than engaging with code and outputs directly; and developers actively manage the collaboration, externalizing plans into persistent artifacts, and negotiating AI autonomy through context injection and behavioral constraints. These results provide foundational empirical insights into AI-assisted development and offer implications for the design of future programming environments.

</details>

*Xiangyang Xiao,Huaxun Huang,Rongxin Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Android apps rely heavily on Data Manipulation Functionalities (DMFs) for handling app-specific data through CRUDS operations, making their correctness vital for reliability. However, detecting Data Manipulation Errors (DMEs) is challenging due to their dependence on specific UI interaction sequences and manifestation as logic bugs. Existing automated UI testing tools face two primary challenges: insufficient UI path coverage for adequate DMF triggering and reliance on manually written test scripts. To address these issues, we propose an automated approach using Large Language Models (LLMs) for DME detection. We developed LDMDroid, an automated UI testing framework for Android apps. LDMDroid enhances DMF triggering success by guiding LLMs through a state-aware process for generating UI event sequences. It also uses visual features to identify changes in data states, improving DME verification accuracy. We evaluated LDMDroid on 24 real-world Android apps, demonstrating improved DMF triggering success rates compared to baselines. LDMDroid discovered 17 unique bugs, with 14 confirmed by developers and 11 fixed. The tool is publicly available at https://github.com/runnnnnner200/LDMDroid.

</details>


### [14] [LibScan: Smart Contract Library Misuse Detection with Iterative Feedback and Static Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00657)
*Yishun Wang,Wenkai Li,Xiaoqi Li,Zongwei Li,Lei Xie,Yuqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LibScan框架，通过结合大语言模型语义推理与规则-based代码分析，自动检测智能合约中第三方代码库的8类误用问题，并引入迭代自校正机制。实验表明该方法在662个真实合约上达到85.15%的检测准确率，显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约中第三方代码库的误用会引入隐藏漏洞，但现有自动化工具难以检测此类问题，因为误用往往需要理解开发者意图而非简单匹配已知代码模式，导致重大经济损失。

Method: 提出LibScan框架：1）融合LLM语义推理与规则-based代码分析的混合检测方法；2）迭代自校正机制，多轮迭代优化分析结果；3）基于大规模实证研究构建结构化知识库。框架覆盖8种不同的库误用类别。

Result: 在662个真实智能合约上的实验表明，LibScan达到85.15%的总体检测准确率，较现有工具提升超过16个百分点。消融实验验证了混合分析方法显著优于单一方法。

Conclusion: LLM语义推理与规则-based代码分析的结合能有效识别智能合约中的库误用问题，迭代自校正机制和结构化知识库可进一步提升检测可靠性。该研究为智能合约安全审计提供了新的自动化检测思路。

Abstract: Smart contracts are self-executing programs that manage financial transactions on blockchain networks. Developers commonly rely on third-party code libraries to improve both efficiency and security. However, improper use of these libraries can introduce hidden vulnerabilities that are difficult to detect, leading to significant financial losses. Existing automated tools struggle to identify such misuse because it often requires understanding the developer's intent rather than simply scanning for known code patterns. This paper presents LibScan, an automated detection framework that combines large language model (LLM)-based semantic reasoning with rule-based code analysis, identifying eight distinct categories of library misuse in smart contracts. To improve detection reliability, the framework incorporates an iterative self-correction mechanism that refines its analysis across multiple rounds, alongside a structured knowledge base derived from large-scale empirical studies of real-world misuse cases. Experiments conducted on 662 real-world smart contracts demonstrate that LibScan achieves an overall detection accuracy of 85.15\%, outperforming existing tools by a margin of over 16 percentage points. Ablation experiments further confirm that combining both analysis approaches yields substantially better results than either method used independently.

</details>


### [15] [Enhancing REST API Fuzzing with Access Policy Violation Checks and Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00702)
*Omur Sahin,Man Zhang,Andrea Arcuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动化安全测试oracle集成到REST API模糊测试工具中，以检测访问策略违规、SQL注入和XSS等安全漏洞，并集成于开源工具EvoMaster中，在52个不同API上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REST API模糊测试工具主要关注检测崩溃（如500 HTTP错误），但安全漏洞对云基础设施可能造成更严重的后果，需要更精细的安全检测机制。

Method: 提出一系列新型自动化oracle，用于检测访问策略违规及执行SQL注入、XSS等传统攻击；将oracle集成到EvoMaster模糊测试工具中，模糊测试完成后执行"安全测试"阶段验证；输出Java、Kotlin、Python、JavaScript多种格式的可执行测试用例。

Result: 在9个人工示例、8个故意存在漏洞的REST API（黑盒测试）和36个WFD语料库API（白盒测试）上进行实验，共52个不同API，结果表明新型oracle及其在模糊测试中的自动化集成可有效检测多个API中的安全问题。

Conclusion: 通过将安全测试oracle集成到模糊测试流程中，可显著提升REST API安全漏洞检测能力，证明了该方法在实际API安全测试中的实用价值。

Abstract: Due to their widespread use in industry, several techniques have been proposed in the literature to fuzz REST APIs. Existing fuzzers for REST APIs have been focusing on detecting crashes (e.g., 500 HTTP server error status code). However, security vulnerabilities can have major drastic consequences on existing cloud infrastructures.
  In this paper, we propose a series of novel automated oracles aimed at detecting violations of access policies in REST APIs, as well as executing traditional attacks such as SQL Injection and XSS. These novel automated oracles can be integrated into existing fuzzers, in which, once the fuzzing session is completed, a ``security testing'' phase is executed to verify these oracles. When a security fault is detected, as output our technique is able to general executable test cases in different formats, like Java, Kotlin, Python and JavaScript test suites.
  Our novel techniques are integrated as an extension of EvoMaster, a state-of-the-art open-source fuzzer for REST APIs. Experiments are carried out on 9 artificial examples, 8 vulnerable-by-design REST APIs with black-box testing, and 36 REST APIs from the WFD corpus with white-box testing, for a total of 52 distinct APIs. Results show that our novel oracles and their automated integration in a fuzzing process can lead to detect security issues in several of these APIs.

</details>


### [16] [The Rise of Language Models in Mining Software Repositories: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00787)
*Miguel Romero-Arjona,Saman Barakat,Ana B. Sánchez,Sergio Segura*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一篇关于语言模型(LMs)在软件仓库挖掘(MSR)领域应用的综合综述，基于对85篇论文的分析，提出了LM在MSR中应用的分类法，识别了关键趋势，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件仓库挖掘(MSR)领域需要分析海量的异构数据，语言模型(LMs)在处理这类任务上展现出越来越强的适应性。自Transformer架构出现以来，LMs已迅速被广泛应用于各类MSR任务中。

Method: 综合综述方法，分析了85篇论文，考察内容包括：LM的应用方式、分析的工件类型、使用的模型、模型采用的演变趋势，以及研究对可重复性和可复用性的支持程度。

Result: 提出了LM在MSR中应用的分类法，识别了塑造该领域的关键趋势，并明确了开放性挑战及可行的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为LM在软件仓库挖掘领域的应用提供了系统性框架，对推动该领域研究的可重复性和可复用性具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: The Mining Software Repositories (MSR) field focuses on analysing the rich data contained in software repositories to derive actionable insights into software processes and products. Mining repositories at scale requires techniques capable of handling large volumes of heterogeneous data, a challenge for which language models (LMs) are increasingly well-suited. Since the advent of Transformer-based architectures, LMs have been rapidly adopted across a wide range of MSR tasks. This article presents a comprehensive survey of the use of LMs in MSR, based on an analysis of 85 papers. We examine how LMs are applied, the types of artefacts analysed, which models are used, how their adoption has evolved over time, and the extent to which studies support reproducibility and reuse. Building on this analysis, we propose a taxonomy of LM applications in MSR, identify key trends shaping the field, and highlight open challenges alongside actionable directions for future research.

</details>


### [17] [Yet Even Less Is Even Better For Agentic, Reasoning, and Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00824)
*Yang Ye,Jingyuan Tan,Tianyue Jiang,Ruizhe Ye,Qiankun He,Jiarui Yang,Jian Dong,Sicong Liang,Chongjian Yue,Peibai Xu,Lufan Lu,Taotao Qian,Junbao Hu,Yuechan Hao,Ensheng Shi,Qi Zhang,Yi Hao,Na Fan,Xin Tan,Shuai Yao,Zhiwei Shen,Zongchen Li,Yanlin Wang,Chong Chen,Yuchi Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STITCH是一个端到端训练框架，通过"粗到细"机制过滤低价值噪声、保留决策关键token，以更少的训练轨迹实现更优的软件工程智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练有效的软件工程智能体需要大量任务特定轨迹，导致高昂的数据构建成本。研究旨在验证"少而精"假说能否扩展至智能体场景。

Method: STITCH（滑动记忆轨迹推断与任务分块启发式）采用粗到细机制，过滤低价值噪声，保留决策关键token以最大化训练信号质量。

Result: SWE-bench Verified上相对提升63.16%；Multi-SWE-bench (Java)达43.75%；HarmonyOS (ArkTS)编译通过率提升至61.31%（+43.34%），仅用不到1K条轨迹。

Conclusion: "少而精"范式可有效泛化至跨语言、跨模型规模的复杂智能体任务。

Abstract: Training effective software engineering agents requires large volumes of task-specific trajectories, incurring substantial data construction costs. Inspired by the "Less-Is-More" hypothesis in mathematical reasoning, we investigate its extension to agentic scenarios and propose an end-to-end training framework that achieves superior agentic capabilities with fewer but higher-quality training trajectories. This is achieved via STITCH (Sliding-memory Trajectory Inference and Task Chunking Heuristic), a coarse-to-fine mechanism that filters low-value noise and retains decision-critical tokens to maximize training signal quality. We conduct experiments across multiple agent frameworks (e.g., mini-SWE-agent, MSWE-agent), model scales (30B to 355B), and multilingual settings (Python, Java, and ArkTS). On SWE-bench Verified, models trained with STITCH achieve up to 63.16% relative improvement over base models. On Multi-SWE-bench (Java), MiniMax-M2.5-STITCH achieves 43.75% with our CodeArts Agent scaffold (+16.67%). On HarmonyOS (ArkTS), GLM-4.7-STITCH improves the compilation pass rate to 61.31% (+43.34%) with less than 1K training trajectories. Our results confirm that the "Less-Is-More" paradigm generalizes effectively to complex agentic tasks across diverse languages and model scales.

</details>


### [18] [Reliability of Large Language Models for Design Synthesis: An Empirical Study of Variance, Prompt Sensitivity, and Method Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00851)
*Rabia Iftikhar,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究LLM是否能超越UML图翻译、执行设计综合，并评估偏好提示方法对设计意图 adherence 的影响。实验涉及540次测试，发现偏好对齐可改善设计意图 adherence，但无法消除非确定性，模型行为对设计可靠性影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM可生成语法正确的UML类图，但语法正确不等于有意义的设计。研究旨在探索LLM是否具备设计综合能力，以及在输入变化时能否保持面向对象原则和设计模式的一致性。

Method: 提出基于偏好的少样本提示方法（preference-based few-shot prompting），在三个LLM（ChatGPT 4o-mini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.5 Flash）上测试三种建模策略（标准提示、规则注入提示、偏好提示）。采用两个设计意图基准测试，每个包含三个领域特定、释义后的提示词，各进行10次重复实验，共540次实验。

Result: 偏好对齐可提升设计意图 adherence，但无法消除非确定性；模型层级行为对设计可靠性有显著影响。

Conclusion: 实现可靠的LLM辅助软件设计，不仅需要有效提示，还需仔细考虑模型行为和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to automate software engineering tasks, including the generation of UML class diagrams from natural language descriptions. While prior work demonstrates that LLMs can produce syntactically valid diagrams, syntactic correctness alone does not guarantee meaningful design. This study investigates whether LLMs can move beyond diagram translation to perform design synthesis, and how reliably they maintain design-oriented reasoning under variation. We introduce a preference-based few-shot prompting approach that biases LLM outputs toward designs satisfying object-oriented principles and pattern-consistent structures. Two design-intent benchmarks, each with three domain-only, paraphrased prompts and 10 repeated runs, are used to evaluate three LLMs (ChatGPT 4o-mini, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Gemini 2.5 Flash) across three modeling strategies: standard prompting, rule-injection prompting, and preference-based prompting, totaling 540 experiments (i.e. 2x3x10x3x3). Results indicate that while preference-based alignment improves adherence to design intent it does not eliminate non-determinism, and model-level behavior strongly influences design reliability. These findings highlight that achieving dependable LLM-assisted software design requires not only effective prompting but also careful consideration of model behavior and robustness.

</details>


### [19] [Detecting Call Graph Unsoundness without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00885)
*Fangtian Zhong,Ollie Wold,Joseph Windmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过大规模实证研究揭示了Java静态分析框架比较中的根本性问题：框架间的语义不一致性。研究分析了Soot、SootUp、WALA和Doop四个主流框架，发现算法精度排序常被打破，配置与算法交互产生协同失效，且不同框架的call-graph语义不可调和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析工具比较研究假设分析算法和配置可以单调组合，且跨工具能产生语义可比的结果，但这一假设从未被系统验证。

Method: 使用精度偏序（precision partial orders）系统地识别违反该假设的情况，通过大规模实证研究在四个Java静态分析框架中进行测试。

Result: 发现三个关键问题：1) 现代语言特性（lambda、反射、native建模）导致框架内算法精度排序频繁打破；2) 配置选择与算法产生协同失效，其影响超过单独变化；3) 跨框架比较揭示不可调和的语义鸿沟，不同框架的call-graph ground truth不兼容。

Conclusion: 挑战了静态分析领域的现行评估实践，强调在评估精度和soundness时需联合考虑算法、配置和框架语义。

Abstract: Java static analysis frameworks are commonly compared under the assumption that analysis algorithms and configurations compose monotonically and yield semantically comparable results across tools. In this work, we show that this assumption is fundamentally flawed. We present a large-scale empirical study of semantic consistency within and across four widely used Java static analysis frameworks: Soot, SootUp, WALA, and Doop. Using precision partial orders over analysis algorithms and configurations, we systematically identify violations where increased precision introduces new call-graph edges or amplifies inconsistencies. Our results reveal three key findings. First, algorithmic precision orders frequently break within frameworks due to modern language features such as lambdas, reflection, and native modeling. Second, configuration choices strongly interact with analysis algorithms, producing synergistic failures that exceed the effects of algorithm or configuration changes alone. Third, cross-framework comparisons expose irreconcilable semantic gaps, demonstrating that different frameworks operate over incompatible notions of call-graph ground truth. These findings challenge prevailing evaluation practices in static analysis and highlight the need to reason jointly about algorithms, configurations, and framework semantics when assessing precision and soundness.

</details>


### [20] [Portable and Secure CI/CD for COBOL: Lessons from an Industrial Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00936)
*Andreas Askholm,Kenneth Johnsen,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bankdata（丹麦银行IT提供商）将基于Jenkins的COBOL CI/CD管道从脆弱、缓慢的架构迁移到容器化架构的工业案例研究，新架构实现了82%的运行时缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业中使用遗留技术时，CI/CD管道本身可能成为技术债务的来源。Bankdata原有的基于Groovy脚本的四仓库架构存在运行时依赖安装、执行时间长、维护成本高和供应商锁定等问题。

Method: 迁移到容器化架构，包括：平台逻辑抽象层、简化仓库结构、预构建的OCI兼容镜像（包含COBOL工具和依赖项）。

Result: 新设计实现了82%的运行时缩减。

Conclusion: 为遗留、高安全环境中的管道现代化提供了关于抽象、容器化和组织采纳的经验教训和指导。

Abstract: Continuous integration and delivery (CI/CD) pipelines are critical for sustaining the evolution of large software systems. In regulated industries with legacy technologies, however, pipelines themselves can become a source of technical debt. This paper presents an industrial case study of Bankdata, a cooperative IT provider for Danish banks, where a Jenkins-based COBOL CI/CD pipeline had grown fragile, slow, and tightly coupled to platform-specific logic. The original architecture relied on Groovy scripts spread across four repositories with runtime dependency installation, leading to long execution times, high maintenance costs, and vendor lock-in.
  We report on the migration to a containerized architecture featuring an abstraction layer for platform logic, simplified repository structure, and a pre-built OCI-compliant image containing COBOL tools and dependencies. The new design achieved an 82% runtime reduction. Our experience highlights lessons on abstraction, containerization, and organizational adoption, offering guidance for modernizing pipelines in legacy, high-security environments.

</details>


### [21] [Revision or Re-Solving? Decomposing Second-Pass Gains in Multi-LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01029)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Chengyu Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文挑战了多LLM修订管道收益主要来源于"真正纠错"的假设。通过控制变量实验，将二阶段收益分解为重解、支架和内容三个成分，在知识密集型MCQ和编程任务上验证，发现收益来源因任务结构、草案质量和草案信息类型而异——MCQ任务中直接路由至更强模型更优，代码生成任务中即使语义空草案也能提供结构支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究普遍假设多LLM修订管道（二阶段：首先生成草案，再由第二模型修订）的收益来源于"真正的错误纠正"，但该假设尚未经过系统验证。研究者认为这种假设可能过于简化。

Method: 设计四组匹配条件，控制变量分离二阶段收益的三个加性成分：重解(re-solving)、支架(scaffold)和内容(content)。在2个模型对上评估3个基准测试（涵盖知识密集型MCQ和竞赛编程）。额外进行角色反转实验。

Result: 收益非单一来源，取决于任务结构、草案质量和草案信息类型。MCQ任务（答案空间受限、草案结构引导有限）：收益主要来自更强模型重解，直接路由优于修订弱草案。代码生成任务：两阶段提示仍有价值，因为语义为空的草案可提供结构支架，而弱草案内容可能有害。角色反转实验表明强草案明显有利于弱审稿者。

Conclusion: 多LLM修订的效用受任务结构和草案质量动态制约。 blanket revision strategies（通用修订策略）非最优，应针对任务类型和草案质量设计差异化管道。

Abstract: Multi-LLM revision pipelines, in which a second model reviews and improves a draft produced by a first, are widely assumed to derive their gains from genuine error correction. We question this assumption with a controlled decomposition experiment that uses four matched conditions to separate second-pass gains into three additive components: re-solving, scaffold, and content. We evaluate this design across two model pairs on three benchmarks spanning knowledge-intensive MCQ and competitive programming. Our results show that the gains of multi-LLM revision are not monolithic, but depend on task structure, draft quality, and the type of draft information. On MCQ tasks, where the answer space is constrained and drafts provide little structural guidance, most gains are consistent with stronger-model re-solving, and directly routing queries to the stronger model can be more effective than revising a weak draft. On code generation tasks, however, two-stage prompting remains useful because even semantically null drafts can provide substantial structural scaffolding, while weak draft content can be harmful. Finally, role-reversed experiments show that strong drafts clearly benefit weak reviewers. Ultimately, our findings demonstrate that the utility of multi-LLM revision is dynamically bottlenecked by task structure and draft quality, necessitating more targeted pipeline designs rather than blanket revision strategies.

</details>


### [22] [Containing the Reproducibility Gap: Automated Repository-Level Containerization for Scholarly Jupyter Notebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01072)
*Sheeba Samuel,Daniel Mietchen,Hemanta Lo,Martin Gaedke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个自动化的可复现性工程管道,用于重构和评估学术notebook的执行环境。通过容器化技术解决了66.7%的依赖相关故障,但53.7%的notebook仍存在低输出保真度问题,表明容器化对计算稳定性必要但对完全比特级可复现性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算可复现性是可信科学的基础,但在实际研究中难以实现。环境漂移、文档缺失的依赖项和隐式执行假设经常阻碍已发表研究的独立重新执行。尽管已有可复现性指南,但缺乏可扩展的系统化自动评估基础设施。

Method: 提出一个自动化的Web导向可复现性工程管道,重构和评估学术notebook的仓库级执行环境。系统执行依赖推断、自动容器生成和隔离执行,以近似notebook的原始计算上下文。在PubMed Central引用116个GitHub仓库的443个notebook上进行评估,将执行结果分为四类:已解决的环境故障、持续的逻辑或数据错误、可复现性漂移、容器引入的回归。

Result: 容器化解决了66.7%的先前依赖相关故障,显著提高了执行鲁棒性。然而,53.7%的notebook表现出低输出保真度,主要由持续的运行时故障和随机非确定性导致。

Conclusion: 标准化容器化对计算稳定性必不可少,但不足以实现完全的比特级可复现性。该框架为研究人员、编辑和档案管理员提供了可扩展的系统化自动计算工件评估解决方案。

Abstract: Computational reproducibility is fundamental to trustworthy science, yet remains difficult to achieve in practice across various research workflows, including Jupyter notebooks published alongside scholarly articles. Environment drift, undocumented dependencies and implicit execution assumptions frequently prevent independent re-execution of published research. Despite existing reproducibility guidelines, scalable and systematic infrastructure for automated assessment remains limited. We present an automated, web-oriented reproducibility engineering pipeline that reconstructs and evaluates repository-level execution environments for scholarly notebooks. The system performs dependency inference, automated container generation, and isolated execution to approximate the notebook's original computational context. We evaluate the approach on 443 notebooks from 116 GitHub repositories referenced by publications in PubMed Central. Execution outcomes are classified into four categories: resolved environment failures, persistent logic or data errors, reproducibility drift, and container-induced regressions. Our results show that containerization resolves 66.7% of prior dependency-related failures and substantially improves execution robustness. However, a significant reproducibility gap remains: 53.7% of notebooks exhibit low output fidelity, largely due to persistent runtime failures and stochastic non-determinism. These findings indicate that standardized containerization is essential for computational stability but insufficient for full bit-wise reproducibility. The framework offers a scalable solution for researchers, editors, and archivists seeking systematic, automated assessment of computational artifacts.

</details>


### [23] [Harnessing Hype to Teach Empirical Thinking: An Experience With AI Coding Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01110)
*Marvin Wyrich,Norman Peitek,Kallistos Weis,Sven Apel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索将AI编程助手这类热门技术融入软件工程教学，是否能帮助学生更好地理解和掌握实证研究方法。结果表明，高热度话题能有效降低实证研究概念的入门门槛，同时结合动手实践和学生主导的探究设计，可显著提升学生的批判性思维和科研能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程学生通常难以理解和欣赏实证方法与假设驱动的研究范式，尤其当这些概念以纯理论方式讲授时，学生参与度和学习效果都不理想。

Method: 开展为期一学期的研讨课，以当前热门的AI编程助手为主题，吸引学生高度关注。课程结合AI编程助手的实践操作与学生自主设计的实证小研究两种形式。

Result: 课堂观察和问卷调查均表明，高热度话题激发了学生的好奇心和批判性思维。学生在使用AI编程助手的同时主动质疑其局限性，形成了评估新兴技术所需的实证思维习惯。

Conclusion: （1）高热度话题能降低实证研究等抽象概念的入门门槛；（2）真实的动手开发任务结合探究自主权可促进批判性参与；（3）单次研讨课能有效同时传授技术技能和科研技能。

Abstract: Software engineering students often struggle to appreciate empirical methods and hypothesis-driven inquiry, especially when taught in theoretical terms. This experience report explores whether grounding empirical learning in hype-driven technologies can make these concepts more accessible and engaging. We conducted a one-semester seminar framed around the currently popular topic of AI coding assistants, which attracted unusually high student interest. The course combined hands-on sessions using AI coding assistants with small, student-designed empirical studies.
  Classroom observations and survey responses suggest that the hype topic sparked curiosity and critical thinking. Students engaged with the AI coding assistants while questioning their limitations -- developing the kind of empirical thinking needed to assess claims about emerging technologies. Key lessons: (1) Hype-driven topics can lower barriers to abstract concepts like empirical research; (2) authentic hands-on development tasks combined with ownership of inquiry foster critical engagement; and (3) a single seminar can effectively teach both technical and research skills.

</details>


### [24] [SERSEM: Selective Entropy-Weighted Scoring for Membership Inference in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01147)
*Kıvanç Kuzey Dikici,Serdar Kara,Semih Çağlar,Eray Tüzün,Sinem Sav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SERSEM框架，通过抑制代码中的语法模板并放大特定记忆信号，对代码大语言模型进行成员推理攻击，在StarCoder2模型上实现AUC-ROC约0.79，优于现有概率基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代码大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地使用大规模、往往许可不明确的数据集进行训练，通过成员推理攻击（MIA）评估数据污染变得至关重要。

Method: SERSEM是一种白盒攻击框架，采用双信号方法：（1）通过静态抽象语法树（AST）分析、基于拼写检查的多语言逻辑检测和离线linting生成连续字符级权重掩码；（2）使用这些启发式权重聚合内部transformer激活并校准输出logits的token级Z分数。

Result: 在25,000样本的平衡数据集上，SERSEM在StarCoder2-3B模型上达到0.7913的全局AUC-ROC，在StarCoder2-7B模型上达到0.7867，持续优于Loss、Min-K% Prob和PAC等概率基准方法。

Conclusion: 聚焦于以人为中心的代码异常比序列级概率平均提供更鲁棒的逐字记忆指标。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) for code increasingly utilize massive, often non-permissively licensed datasets, evaluating data contamination through Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) has become critical. We propose SERSEM (Selective Entropy-Weighted Scoring for Membership Inference), a novel white-box attack framework that suppresses uninformative syntactical boilerplate to amplify specific memorization signals. SERSEM utilizes a dual-signal methodology: first, a continuous character-level weight mask is derived through static Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) analysis, spellchecking-based multilingual logic detection, and offline linting. Second, these heuristic weights are used to pool internal transformer activations and calibrate token-level Z-scores from the output logits. Evaluated on a 25,000-sample balanced dataset, SERSEM achieves a global AUC-ROC of 0.7913 on the StarCoder2-3B model and 0.7867 on the StarCoder2-7B model, consistently outperforming the implemented probability-based baselines Loss, Min-K% Prob, and PAC. Our findings demonstrate that focusing on human-centric coding anomalies provides a significantly more robust indicator of verbatim memorization than sequence-level probability averages.

</details>


### [25] [Automated Generation of High-Quality Bug Reports for Android Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01148)
*Antu Saha,Atish Kumar Dipongkor,Sam Bennett,Kevin Moran,Andrian Marcus,Oscar Chaparro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BugScribe利用大语言模型（LLM）结合GUI交互和屏幕上下文信息，自动生成高质量的移动应用缺陷报告（包含观察行为、预期行为和复现步骤）。通过48个来自26个Android应用的缺陷报告进行评估，结果表明该方法生成的报告质量优于原始报告和现有LLM基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用中的大多数缺陷是视觉可观察的，用户和测试人员需要手动提交缺陷报告。然而，这些报告经常存在歧义或不准确的问题，常常缺少关键组件如观察行为（OB）、预期行为（EB）或复现步骤（S2Rs）。低质量的报告阻碍开发者理解和复现缺陷，导致修复延迟或错误。

Method: BugScribe将特定的应用相关信息（如GUI交互和缺陷出现的特定屏幕）作为上下文提供给LLM，自动生成清晰、详细且准确的OB、EB和S2Rs。研究团队构建了一个统一的quality框架，用于定义OB、EB和S2Rs的正确性和完整性维度。

Result: 使用来自26个Android应用的48个缺陷报告进行评估，结果表明BugScribe生成的报告在质量和准确性上均优于原始报告，并超过了近期的LLM基准方法。

Conclusion: BugScribe可作为测试人员和开发者的实用助手，通过提供可靠准确的OB、EB和S2Rs来增强不完整的缺陷报告，从而简化缺陷修复流程并提升移动应用质量。

Abstract: Most defects in mobile applications are visually observable on the device screen. To track these defects, users, testers, and developers must manually submit bug reports, especially in the absence of crashes. However, these reports are frequently ambiguous or inaccurate, often omitting essential components such as the Observed Behavior (OB), Expected Behavior (EB), or Steps to Reproduce (S2Rs). Low-quality reports hinder developers' ability to understand and reproduce defects, delaying resolution and leading to incorrect or unresolvable fixes.
  In this paper, we posit that providing specific app-related information (e.g., GUI interactions or specific screens where bugs appear) to LLMs as key points of context can assist in automatically generating clear, detailed, and accurate OB, EB, and S2Rs. We built and evaluated a novel approach, BugScribe, that generates bug reports in this way. To support the evaluation, we introduce a unified quality framework that defines correctness and completeness dimensions for OB, EB, and S2Rs. Using 48 bug reports from 26 Android apps, we show that BugScribe produces higher-quality and more accurate components than the original reports and outperforms recent LLM-based baselines. We envision that BugScribe can serve as a practical assistant for testers and developers by enhancing incomplete bug reports with reliable and accurate OB, EB, and S2Rs, thereby streamlining bug resolution and improving mobile app quality.

</details>

### [26] [Two-Stage Optimizer-Aware Online Data Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00001)
*Fangxin Wang,Peyman Baghershahi,Langzhou He,Henry Peng Zou,Sourav Medya,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向 LLM 在线微调的优化器感知梯度数据选择与重加权框架，将在线选择建模为优化器状态下的更新匹配问题，并设计了 Filter-then-Weight 两阶段算法，在长上下文数据场景下通过分解外积梯度表示提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的数据选择方法主要针对离线场景设计，难以适应 LLM 在线微调中数据顺序到达、样本效用随训练步变化、以及自适应优化器塑造更新几何的动态特性。现有方法缺乏对优化器状态的显式建模，导致在在线设置下表现受限。

Method: (1) 将在线数据选择形式化为优化器感知的目标更新匹配问题，建立其与二阶目标效用的理论联系；(2) 证明子集层面的构建必须考虑样本间的交互与冗余；(3) 设计两阶段 Filter-then-Weight 算法：先过滤几何上有用的候选样本，再优化其系数；(4) 为适配 LLM 引入分解外积梯度表示与优化的矩阵计算以处理长上下文数据。

Result: 实验表明，在相同数据预算下，该方法相比现有在线数据选择基线方法持续提升了收敛速度和下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 通过将优化器状态纳入数据选择决策，所提框架能更有效地在在线微调场景下识别高价值样本，为 LLM 高效数据选择提供了兼具理论依据和实践可行性的新范式。

Abstract: Gradient-based data selection offers a principled framework for estimating sample utility in large language model (LLM) fine-tuning, but existing methods are mostly designed for offline settings. They are therefore less suited to online fine-tuning, where data arrives sequentially, sample utility is step-dependent, and the effective update geometry is shaped by adaptive optimizers. We propose an optimizer-aware framework for gradient-based online data selection and reweighting in LLM fine-tuning. Our key idea is to view online selection not as static sample ranking, but as shaping the next target-oriented update under the optimizer state. We formulate this as an optimizer-aware update-matching problem, establish its connection to second-order target utility, and show why subset-level construction must account for interactions and redundancy among selected samples. Based on this view, we develop a two-stage Filter-then-Weight algorithm that first filters geometrically useful candidates and then optimizes their coefficients. To make the framework practical for LLMs, we introduce a factorized outer-product gradient representation and optimized matrix computations for long-context data. Experiments show that our method consistently improves convergence and downstream performance over existing online data selection baselines under the same data budget.

</details>


### [27] [Task-Centric Personalized Federated Fine-Tuning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00050)
*Gabriel U. Talasso,Meghdad Kurmanji,Allan M. de Souza,Nicholas D. Lane,Leandro A. Villas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedRouter是一种基于聚类的个性化联邦学习框架，通过适配器为每个任务（而非每个客户端）构建专业化模型，解决异构任务聚合时的性能下降、泛化能力不足和任务干扰问题，在任务干扰场景下提升6.1%，泛化评估中提升136%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在聚合异构任务的模型时往往导致各客户端性能下降；现有个性化联邦学习方法缺乏鲁棒性，主要体现在两方面：(1)泛化能力弱——面对未见任务或数据分布变化时表现差；(2)任务干扰问题——单个客户端数据含多种分布，本地训练时相互干扰。

Method: 提出FedRouter框架，采用双层聚类机制：(1)本地聚类——将适配器与任务数据样本关联；(2)全局聚类——聚合不同客户端的相似适配器构建以任务为中心的个性化模型。同时设计评估路由机制，根据聚类结果将测试样本路由至最优适配器。

Result: FedRouter在多任务数据集上表现优异：任务干扰场景下相对提升6.1%，泛化评估中相对提升136%。

Conclusion: FedRouter通过任务中心化而非客户端中心化的个性化策略，有效解决了异构联邦学习中的泛化性和任务干扰问题，为分布式隐私保护环境下的多任务学习提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising technique for training language models on distributed and private datasets of diverse tasks. However, aggregating models trained on heterogeneous tasks often degrades the overall performance of individual clients. To address this issue, Personalized FL (pFL) aims to create models tailored for each client's data distribution. Although these approaches improve local performance, they usually lack robustness in two aspects: (i) generalization: when clients must make predictions on unseen tasks, or face changes in their data distributions, and (ii) intra-client tasks interference: when a single client's data contains multiple distributions that may interfere with each other during local training. To tackle these two challenges, we propose FedRouter, a clustering-based pFL that builds specialized models for each task rather than for each client. FedRouter uses adapters to personalize models by employing two clustering mechanisms to associate adapters with specific tasks. A local clustering that associate adapters with task data samples and a global one that associates similar adapters from different clients to construct task-centric personalized models. Additionally, we propose an evaluation router mechanism that routes test samples to the best adapter based on the created clusters. Experiments comparing our method with existing approaches across a multitask dataset, FedRouter demonstrate strong resilience in these challenging scenarios performing up to 6.1% relatively better under tasks interference and up to 136% relative improvement under generalization evaluation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文对比了进化策略(ES)与深度强化学习(DRL)在不同难度任务上的表现，并探索ES能否作为DRL的预训练手段。结果表明ES并未普遍快于DRL；ES预训练仅在简单环境(Flappy Bird)中有效，在复杂环境(Breakout、MuJoCo Walker)中无明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽然效果显著，但计算资源消耗大、参数调优复杂；进化策略作为更简单、免梯度的替代方案，其性能差距尚不明确，且是否能为DRL提供有益的初始训练策略仍有待验证。

Method: 在不同难度任务（Flappy Bird、Breakout、MuJoCo Walker）上分别运行ES和DRL算法进行对比；设计实验将ES作为预训练阶段，观察其对后续DRL训练效率的影响。

Result: ES在训练速度上并不一致地优于DRL；ES预训练仅在简单环境中带来好处，在复杂任务上对训练效率或稳定性的改善微乎其微甚至没有。

Conclusion: ES难以替代DRL用于复杂决策任务，也不适合作为复杂环境下的DRL预训练策略；其简单性优势主要体现在简单场景中。

Abstract: Although Deep Reinforcement Learning has proven highly effective for complex decision-making problems, it demands significant computational resources and careful parameter adjustment in order to develop successful strategies. Evolution strategies offer a more straightforward, derivative-free approach that is less computationally costly and simpler to deploy. However, ES generally do not match the performance levels achieved by DRL, which calls into question their suitability for more demanding scenarios. This study examines the performance of ES and DRL across tasks of varying difficulty, including Flappy Bird, Breakout and Mujoco environments, as well as whether ES could be used for initial training to enhance DRL algorithms. The results indicate that ES do not consistently train faster than DRL. When used as a preliminary training step, they only provide benefits in less complex environments (Flappy Bird) and show minimal or no improvement in training efficiency or stability across different parameter settings when applied to more sophisticated tasks (Breakout and MuJoCo Walker).

</details>


### [29] [MAC-Attention: a Match-Amend-Complete Scheme for Fast and Accurate Attention Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00235)
*Jinghan Yao,Sam Adé Jacobs,Walid Krichene,Masahiro Tanaka,Dhabaleswar K Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAC-Attention通过复用近期相似查询的先前注意力计算来加速LLM长上下文解码，在不损失保真度和访问完整性的前提下，将KV访问降低99%，在128K上下文时延迟降低60%以上，端到端加速达2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文解码是IO密集型任务，每个token都需要重新读取不断增长的KV缓存。现有的加速方法如压缩（损害保真度）或选择/淘汰（限制可访问性）都会影响延迟召回和长文本生成质量。

Method: MAC-Attention包含三个阶段：1）匹配阶段：对短局部窗口进行RoPE前L2匹配；2）修正阶段：重新计算匹配边界附近的小带宽区域；3）完成阶段：通过数值稳定融合将修正结果与KV尾部的新的注意力计算合并。该方法与IO感知内核、分页KV管理器、MQA/GQA兼容。

Result: 在LongBench v2 (120K)、RULER (120K)、LongGenBench (16K连续生成)上，相比最新FlashInfer库，MAC-Attention将KV访问降低99%，128K时token生成延迟降低60%以上，注意力阶段加速超过14.3倍，端到端加速达2.6倍，同时保持全注意力质量。

Conclusion: MAC-Attention通过计算复用实现了快速且保真的长上下文推理，为LLM长上下文场景提供了一种无需牺牲精度或访问完整性的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context decoding in LLMs is IO-bound: each token re-reads an ever-growing KV cache. Prior accelerations cut bytes via compression, which lowers fidelity, or selection/eviction, which restricts what remains accessible, and both can degrade delayed recall and long-form generation. We introduce MAC-Attention, a fidelity- and access-preserving alternative that accelerates decoding by reusing prior attention computations for semantically similar recent queries. It starts with a match stage that performs pre-RoPE L2 matching over a short local window; an amend stage rectifies the reused attention by recomputing a small band near the match boundary; and a complete stage fuses the rectified results with fresh attention computed on the KV tail through a numerically stable merge. On a match hit, the compute and bandwidth complexity is constant regardless of context length. The method is model-agnostic and composes with IO-aware kernels, paged-KV managers, and MQA/GQA. Across LongBench v2 (120K), RULER (120K), and LongGenBench (16K continuous generation), compared to the latest FlashInfer library, MAC-Attention reduces KV accesses by up to 99%, cuts token generation latency by over 60% at 128K, and achieves over 14.3x attention-phase speedups, up to 2.6x end-to-end, while maintaining full-attention quality. By reusing computation, MAC-Attention delivers long-context inference that is both fast and faithful. Code is available here: https://github.com/YJHMITWEB/MAC-Attention.git

</details>

*Dharma Teja Vooturi,Dhiraj Kalamkar,Dipankar Das,Bharat Kaul*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头预训练LLM需要海量算力，本文旨在展示在Aurora超算上实现千GPU级别的大规模LLM预训练能力，解决超大规模分布式训练的效率、可靠性和容错问题。

Method: 开发Optimus训练库，支持标准大模型训练技术；使用3072-12288 GPU tiles进行预训练；采用自定义GPU内核加速MoE专家计算；设计EP-Aware分片优化器；在OLMoE-mix-0924数据集上训练。

Result: 成功预训练Mula-1B（dense）和多个MoE模型至7B/20B/100B/220B规模；12288 GPU tiles下扩展效率约90%；MoE模型训练加速达1.71x。

Conclusion: Aurora超算能够高效支持千亿参数级别LLM的预训练，Optimus库提供了可靠的分布式训练解决方案，MoE架构配合定制优化可实现接近线性扩展。

Abstract: Pretraining Large Language Models (LLMs) from scratch requires massive amount of compute. Aurora super computer is an ExaScale machine with 127,488 Intel PVC (Ponte Vechio) GPU tiles. In this work, we showcase LLM pretraining on Aurora at the scale of 1000s of GPU tiles. Towards this effort, we developed Optimus, an inhouse training library with support for standard large model training techniques. Using Optimus, we first pretrained Mula-1B, a 1 Billion dense model and Mula-7B-A1B, a 7 Billion Mixture of Experts (MoE) model from scratch on 3072 GPU tiles for the full 4 trillion tokens of the OLMoE-mix-0924 dataset. We then demonstrated model scaling by pretraining three large MoE models Mula-20B-A2B, Mula-100B-A7B, and Mula-220B-A10B till 100 Billion tokens on the same dataset. On our largest model Mula-220B-A10B, we pushed the compute scaling from 384 to 12288 GPU tiles and observed scaling efficiency of around 90% at 12288 GPU tiles. We significantly improved the runtime performance of MoE models using custom GPU kernels for expert computation, and a novel EP-Aware sharded optimizer resulting in training speedups up to 1.71x. As part of the Optimus library, we also developed a robust set of reliability and fault tolerant features to improve training stability and continuity at scale.

</details>

Deringer,Werner Dobrautz,Adrian Ehrenhofer,Thorben Frank,Pascal Friederich,Rico Friedrich,Janine George,Luca Ghiringhelli,Alejandra Hinostroza Caldas,Veronika Juraskova,Hannes Kneiding,Yury Lysogorskiy,Johannes T. Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming molecular and materials science, but its growing computational and data demands raise critical sustainability challenges. In this Perspective, we examine resource considerations across the AI-driven discovery pipeline--from quantum-mechanical (QM) data generation and model training to automated, self-driving research workflows--building on discussions from the ``SusML workshop: Towards sustainable exploration of chemical spaces with machine learning'' held in Dresden, Germany. In this context, the availability of large quantum datasets has enabled rigorous benchmarking and rapid methodological progress, while also incurring substantial energy and infrastructure costs. We highlight emerging strategies to enhance efficiency, including general-purpose machine learning (ML) models, multi-fidelity approaches, model distillation, and active learning. Moreover, incorporating physics-based constraints within hierarchical workflows, where fast ML surrogates are applied broadly and high-accuracy QM methods are used selectively, can further optimize resource use without compromising reliability. Equally important is bridging the gap between idealized computational predictions and real-world conditions by accounting for synthesizability and multi-objective design criteria, which is essential for practical impact. Finally, we argue that sustainable progress will rely on open data and models, reusable workflows, and domain-specific AI systems that maximize scientific value per unit of computation, enabling efficient and responsible discovery of technological materials and therapeutics.

### [32] [ParetoBandit: Budget-Paced Adaptive Routing for Non-Stationary LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00136)
*Annette Taberner-Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParetoBandit是一个开源的自适应LLM路由系统，基于成本感知的情境老虎机算法，首次同时实现了美元计价的预算约束、在线适应环境变化、运行时模型热插拔三大功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境LLM服务通常使用跨越约530倍成本范围的多模型组合，路由决策需在质量与成本间权衡。挑战在于：定价动态变化、模型质量可能静默退化、新模型需无停机集成——现有方案难以同时解决这些问题。

Method: 三大核心机制：(1) 在线原始-对偶预算调节器，对开放流实施每请求成本上限；(2) 充分统计量的几何遗忘，实现对价格和质量变化的快速适应；(3) 热插拔注册表，支持运行时添加/移除模型，包含短暂强制探索阶段。

Result: 在1824个提示词、三模型组合场景下：平均每请求成本不超过目标0.4%；价格骤降10倍时质量提升0.071；静默质量退化被检测并重新路由；冷启动模型约142步达到有意义采用；CPU上端到端延迟9.8ms，决策本身仅22.5μs。

Conclusion: ParetoBandit能有效实现成本-质量帕累托最优的动态路由，在非平稳环境下保持预算约束的同时区分对待不同模型，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Production LLM serving often relies on multi-model portfolios spanning a ~530x cost range, where routing decisions trade off quality against cost. This trade-off is non-stationary: providers revise pricing, model quality can regress silently, and new models must be integrated without downtime. We present ParetoBandit, an open-source adaptive router built on cost-aware contextual bandits that is the first to simultaneously enforce dollar-denominated budgets, adapt online to such shifts, and onboard new models at runtime.
  ParetoBandit closes these gaps through three mechanisms. An online primal-dual budget pacer enforces a per-request cost ceiling over an open-ended stream, replacing offline penalty tuning with closed-loop control. Geometric forgetting on sufficient statistics enables rapid adaptation to price and quality shifts while bootstrapping from offline priors. A hot-swap registry lets operators add or remove models at runtime, with a brief forced-exploration phase for each newcomer, after which UCB selection discovers its quality-cost niche from live traffic alone.
  We evaluate ParetoBandit across four deployment scenarios on 1,824 prompts routed through a three-model portfolio. Across seven budget ceilings, mean per-request cost never exceeds the target by more than 0.4%. When conditions shift, the system adapts: an order-of-magnitude price cut on the costliest model yields up to +0.071 quality lift, and a silent quality regression is detected and rerouted within budget. A cold-started model reaches meaningful adoption within ~142 steps without breaching the cost ceiling. The router discriminates rather than blindly adopting: expensive models are budget-gated and low-quality models rejected after bounded exploration. End-to-end routing latency is 9.8ms on CPU -- less than 0.4% of typical inference time -- with the routing decision itself taking just 22.5us.

</details>


### [33] [Sit-to-Stand Transitions Detection and Duration Measurement Using Smart Lacelock Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00175)
*Md Rafi Islam,Md Rejwanul Haque,Elizabeth Choma,Shannon Hayes,Siobhan McMahon,Xiangrong Shen,Edward Sazonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于Smart Lacelock传感器的坐站转换(SiSt)检测和持续时间测量方法，通过机器学习分类器对16名老年人进行评估。袋决策树分类器达到0.98的准确率和0.8的F1分数，正确分类转换的持续时间测量平均绝对误差为0.047秒，显示出该传感器在老年人跌倒风险评估和活动监测方面的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人群体面临与年龄相关的平衡能力、肌肉力量和活动能力下降问题，增加了跌倒风险。坐站转换(SiSt)是评估下肢力量、肌肉骨骼健康和跌倒风险的关键指标，因此需要一种可靠的日常监测方法。

Method: 研究使用Smart Lacelock传感器(集成载荷传感器、加速度计和陀螺仪)采集信号，从多模态信号中提取特征，训练并评估四种机器学习分类器，采用4折参与者独立交叉验证进行分类和持续时间测量。

Result: 袋决策树分类器在SiSt转换分类中达到0.98的准确率和0.8的F1分数；正确分类转换的持续时间测量平均绝对误差为0.047秒，标准差为0.07秒。

Conclusion: Smart Lacelock传感器具有在真实世界中进行老年人跌倒风险评估和活动监测的潜力，可用于日常功能能力评估和身体衰退监测。

Abstract: Postural stability during movement is fundamental to independent living, fall prevention, and overall health, particularly among older adults who experience age-related declines in balance, muscle strength, and mobility. Among daily functional activities, the Sit-to-Stand (SiSt) transition is a critical indicator of lower-limb strength, musculoskeletal health, and fall risk, making it an essential parameter for assessing functional capacity and monitoring physical decline in aging populations. This study presents a methodology SiSt transition detection and duration measurement using the Smart Lacelock sensor, a lightweight, shoe-mounted device that integrates a load cell, accelerometer, and gyroscope for motion analysis. The methodology was evaluated in 16 older adults (age: mean: 76.84, SD: 3.45 years) performing SiSt tasks within the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) protocol. Features extracted from multimodal signals were used to train and evaluate four machine learning classifiers using a 4-fold participant-independent cross-validation to classify SiSt transitions and measure their duration. The bagged tree classifier achieved an accuracy of 0.98 and an F1 score of 0.8 in classifying SiSt transition. The mean absolute error in duration measurement of the correctly classified transitions was 0.047, and the SD was 0.07 seconds. These findings highlight the potential of the Smart Lacelock sensor for real-world fall-risk assessment and mobility monitoring in older adults.

</details>


### [34] [Lipschitz Dueling Bandits over Continuous Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00523)
*Mudit Sharma,Shweta Jain,Vaneet Aggarwal,Ganesh Ghalme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了具有Lipschitz结构的连续动作空间上的随机决斗老虎机问题，其中反馈仅为比较性（comparative）反馈。文章提出了首个针对Lipschitz决斗老虎机的算法，采用基于轮次的探索和基于自适应参考臂的递归区域消除方法。理论分析证明了regret bound为$\tilde O(T^{\frac{d_z+1}{d_z+2}})$，其中$d_z$为近优区域的缩放维度（zooming dimension）。此外，该算法仅需对数级别的空间复杂度，这在连续动作空间老虎机算法中是最优可达的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决斗老虎机（dueling bandits）和Lipschitz老虎机此前已被分别研究，但二者的结合尚未被探索。现实应用中，如推荐系统中用户偏好往往是比较性的（如A比B更好），而非绝对评分，同时动作空间具有连续性和Lipschitz平滑结构（如位置、特征空间）。因此，有必要研究结合这两种特性的新型老虎机问题，以更好地建模实际场景。

Method: 本文提出首个Lipschitz决斗老虎机算法，主要技术特点包括：1）基于轮次（round-based）的探索机制；2）递归区域消除（recursive region elimination）策略；3）自适应参考臂（adaptive reference arm）引导搜索方向。通过设计专门处理相对反馈的分析工具，实现对近优区域的逐步定位与细化。

Result: 理论证明该算法达到$\tilde O(T^{\frac{d_z+1}{d_z+2}})$的遗憾界，其中$d_z$为近优区域的zooming dimension。当$d_z=0$时（如孤立最优点），该界退化为对数级$\tilde O(\log T)$。空间复杂度仅为$O(\log T)$，这在连续动作空间老虎机中是最佳可达的。

Conclusion: 本文首次完整解决了Lipschitz结构与决斗反馈的结合问题，填补了老虎机理论研究的重要空白。算法在理论上达到了最优的 regret-space trade-off，对连续空间中的比较式交互学习问题具有重要的理论价值和应用前景。

Abstract: We study for the first time, stochastic dueling bandits over continuous action spaces with Lipschitz structure, where feedback is purely comparative. While dueling bandits and Lipschitz bandits have been studied separately, their combination has remained unexplored. We propose the first algorithm for Lipschitz dueling bandits, using round-based exploration and recursive region elimination guided by an adaptive reference arm. We develop new analytical tools for relative feedback and prove a regret bound of $\tilde O\left(T^{\frac{d_z+1}{d_z+2}}\right)$, where $d_z$ is the zooming dimension of the near-optimal region. Further, our algorithm takes only logarithmic space in terms of the total time horizon, best achievable by any bandit algorithm over a continuous action space.

</details>


### [35] [Lévy-Flow Models: Heavy-Tail-Aware Normalizing Flows for Financial Risk Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00195)
*Rachid Drissi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lévy-Flows是一种新型归一化流模型，使用Lévy过程分布（VG和NIG）替代标准高斯分布，能有效捕捉重尾行为，在金融收益建模中相比高斯流测试NLL降低69%，并实现精确的VaR校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准归一化流模型依赖高斯分布作为基础分布，但金融数据普遍呈现重尾特性，高斯分布难以准确捕捉极端事件。现有方法在处理重尾数据时存在精度与计算效率的矛盾。

Method: 提出Lévy-Flows框架，核心创新：(1)采用Variance Gamma (VG)和Normal-Inverse Gaussian (NIG)作为基础分布，保留精确似然计算和高效重参数化采样；(2)建立理论保证，证明正则变化基础分布在渐近线性流变换下尾指数保持不变；(3)Identity-tail Neural Spline Flow架构在变换区域外精确保持基础分布尾形。

Result: VG流相比高斯流测试负对数似然降低69%，精确实现95% VaR校准；NIG流提供最准确的预期 shortfall估计。在S&P 500日收益及其他资产上的实验表明，融入Lévy过程结构的归一化流在密度估计和风险校准方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Lévy-Flows成功将Lévy过程结构融入归一化流框架，理论上保证尾行为、可精确计算似然，为金融风险管理中重尾数据建模提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We introduce Lévy-Flows, a class of normalizing flow models that replace the standard Gaussian base distribution with Lévy process-based distributions, specifically Variance Gamma (VG) and Normal-Inverse Gaussian (NIG). These distributions naturally capture heavy-tailed behavior while preserving exact likelihood evaluation and efficient reparameterized sampling. We establish theoretical guarantees on tail behavior, showing that for regularly varying bases the tail index is preserved under asymptotically linear flow transformations, and that identity-tail Neural Spline Flow architectures preserve the base distribution's tail shape exactly outside the transformation region. Empirically, we evaluate on S&P 500 daily returns and additional assets, demonstrating substantial improvements in density estimation and risk calibration. VG-based flows reduce test negative log-likelihood by 69% relative to Gaussian flows and achieve exact 95% VaR calibration, while NIG-based flows provide the most accurate Expected Shortfall estimates. These results show that incorporating Lévy process structure into normalizing flows yields significant gains in modeling heavy-tailed data, with applications to financial risk management.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer中的注意力机制使用缩放点积和softmax操作，但查询和键向量的范数可能任意增大，导致训练不稳定，尤其是在数据中存在易学习的虚假模式时问题更为突出。

Method: 提出QUEST方法：1) 将键向量约束到超球面潜在空间，限制其范数；2) 允许单个令牌灵活控制注意力分布的锐度。QUEST可作为标准注意力的直接替代方案。

Result: 实验表明QUEST：(1) 训练过程稳定无不稳定现象；(2) 模型性能提升；(3) 对数据损坏和对抗攻击具有鲁棒性。方法在视觉任务中得到验证，同时探索了其他领域以证明其通用性。

Conclusion: QUEST通过超球面约束有效解决了Transformer训练中的不稳定问题，同时保持了模型的表达能力和灵活性，是一种实用且广泛适用的注意力机制改进方案。

Abstract: The Transformer model architecture has become one of the most widely used in deep learning and the attention mechanism is at its core. The standard attention formulation uses a softmax operation applied to a scaled dot product between query and key vectors. We explore the role played by norms of the queries and keys, which can cause training instabilities when they arbitrarily increase. We demonstrate how this can happen even in simple Transformer models, in the presence of easy-to-learn spurious patterns in the data. We propose a new attention formulation, QUEry-modulated Spherical aTtention (QUEST), that constrains the keys to a hyperspherical latent space, while still allowing individual tokens to flexibly control the sharpness of the attention distribution. QUEST can be easily used as a drop-in replacement for standard attention. We focus on vision applications while also exploring other domains to highlight the method's generality. We show that (1) QUEST trains without instabilities and (2) produces models with improved performance (3) that are robust to data corruptions and adversarial attacks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习应用中性能与安全性/公平性之间的权衡问题，特别是在需要满足受保护群体福利约束的场景（如公平性约束、安全性约束）。现有方法缺乏对统计不确定性如何影响约束满足的理论分析。

Method: 1) 从参考策略下收集的成对比较中，通过最大似然估计 oracle 特异性奖励；2) 分析统计不确定性如何在拉格朗日对偶程序中传播；3) 将约束目标表述为以 Gibbs 策略为原始优化器的 KL 正则化拉格朗日形式；4) 将学习问题转化为凸对偶问题；5) 提出仅需优化对偶变量的算法。

Result: 1) 算法能以高概率保证约束满足；2) 提供首个离线约束偏好学习的有限样本性能界；3) 理论分析可扩展至多约束和一般 f-散度正则化场景。

Conclusion: 本文为离线约束偏好学习提供了首个有限样本理论保证，验证了 KL 正则化拉格朗日框架在处理多约束场景和统计不确定性时的有效性，为安全关键的 RLHF 应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study offline constrained reinforcement learning from human feedback with multiple preference oracles. Motivated by applications that trade off performance with safety or fairness, we aim to maximize target population utility subject to a minimum protected group welfare constraint. From pairwise comparisons collected under a reference policy, we estimate oracle-specific rewards via maximum likelihood and analyze how statistical uncertainty propagates through the dual program. We cast the constrained objective as a KL-regularized Lagrangian whose primal optimizer is a Gibbs policy, reducing learning to a convex dual problem. We propose a dual-only algorithm that ensures high-probability constraint satisfaction and provide the first finite-sample performance guarantees for offline constrained preference learning. Finally, we extend our theoretical analysis to accommodate multiple constraints and general f-divergence regularization.

</details>

*Thomas Buckley,Leslie Schumm,Manor Askenazi,Edward Rietman*

  <summary>Details</summary>
