### [93] [Fiber-Navigable Search: A Geometric Approach to Filtered ANN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00102)
*Thuong Dang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于过滤式近似最近邻（ANN）搜索的几何框架，通过分析过滤后子图（fiber）的几何特性，设计两阶段搜索算法，并识别三种搜索失败模式（拓扑切割、几何折叠、真实盆地），通过锚点结构实现失败恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过滤操作会显著改变图的连通性和几何结构，导致传统ANN方法性能下降。现有方法未能充分考虑过滤后子图的独特几何特性。

Method: 提出两阶段搜索算法：结合全图探索与过滤邻域下降。利用局部信号分类搜索失败为三种模式，并引入轻量级锚点结构识别适合重启的fiber-present聚类区域。

Result: 方法在过滤搜索任务上优于FAISS HNSW。三种失败模式能够清晰分离且随过滤选择性可预测转移。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角理解过滤ANN搜索问题，统一的重启策略可有效解决不同类型的搜索失败，为过滤搜索提供了理论框架和实用方案。

Abstract: We present a geometric framework for filtered approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search. Filtering a proximity graph by a metadata predicate produces a subgraph, a fiber, whose connectivity and geometry can differ sharply from the full graph. Using local signals, we propose a two-phase search algorithm that combines full-graph exploration with filtered-neighbor descent when the local geometry is favorable. These signals also classify search failures into three regimes: topological cuts, geometric folds, and genuine basins. A key observation is that all three share a common resolution: restarting the search in a fiber-present cluster near the query. To support this, we introduce a lightweight anchor structure that identifies such regions and restarts the search accordingly. We show empirically that the method outperforms FAISS HNSW on filtered search and the three failure regimes separate cleanly and shift predictably with filter selectivity.

</details>


### [94] [Reasoning about Transactional Isolation Levels with Isolde](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00159)
*Manuel Barros,Alcino Cunha,Jose Pereira,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Isolde工具，能够自动生成区分不同数据库隔离级别的反例，支持定义等价性检查和属性验证，并成功复现了经典结论且发现了现有规范中的未知bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据库隔离级别的形式化框架已经存在，但理解和比较不同规范之间的语义差异仍然非常困难。现有方法缺乏有效工具来直观展示隔离级别间的细微差别。

Method: Isolde工具通过自动生成"某隔离级别允许但另一隔离级别不允许"的执行示例，作为核心原语来区分不同隔离级别。该方法可应用于检查定义等价性、验证（通过反驳）隔离级别的语义属性。

Result: Isolde能够轻松自动地复现文献中著名的难觅结论，并在最先进的隔离级别检查器的替代规范中发现了一个此前未知的bug，验证了其有效性和实用价值。

Conclusion: 该工具为隔离级别研究提供了有效的自动化验证手段，证明了自动生成反例在形式化验证中的实用价值，为隔离级别的规范开发和验证开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Most databases can be configured to operate under isolation levels weaker than serializability. These enforce fewer restrictions on the concurrent access to data and consequently allow for more performant implementations. While formal frameworks for rigorously specifying isolation levels exist, reasoning about the semantic differences between specifications remains notoriously difficult.
  This paper proposes a tool -- Isolde -- that can automatically generate examples that are allowed by an isolation level but disallowed by another. This simple primitive unlocks a range of useful reasoning tasks, including checking equivalence between definitions, and verifying (by refutation) subtle semantic properties of isolation levels. For example, Isolde allowed us to easily and automatically reproduce a famously elusive result from the literature and to discover a previously unknown bug in the alternative specification of a standard isolation level used in a state-of-the-art isolation checker.

</details>


### [95] [The Data Hydration Gap: A Formal Model of Underinvestment in General-Purpose Data Products Under Decentralized Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00218)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文研究了数据网格范式下的治理困境：将数据产品所有权去中心化后，各领域团队仅优化自身分析需求，导致跨域复用性投资不足。作者将其建模为N个领域同时选择质量(q)和通用性(g)的博弈，证明纳什均衡的通用性缺口随领域数量和跨域分析价值增加而扩大，在某些参数配置下会出现"数据网格陷阱"——即没有可复用的银层自然产生，技术债以N²速度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据网格范式中，去中心化数据所有权导致各领域团队仅关注自身即时分析需求，对创造跨域复用的通用性投入不足，形成负和博弈。缺乏形式化框架来理解不同治理模式（中心化、联邦式、混合式）的适用条件和权衡。

Method: 将跨域数据治理投资问题形式化为同时行动博弈：N个领域同时选择质量(q)和通用性(g)，其中通用性产生正外部性但对个体而言成本高昂。通过求解纳什均衡、分析比较静态效应、进行校准估算来评估均衡结果。

Result: 纳什均衡的通用性缺口随领域数量N和跨域分析价值单调递增；存在"数据网格陷阱"——无 reusable silver layer 自行产生的角点解；技术债以N²速度增长；在合理企业参数下存在显著的组织福利损失。

Conclusion: 模型给出了中心化、联邦式和混合治理各自占优的模型内条件；识别了影响实施的信息不对称和交易成本；为去中心化数据治理的实证研究提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: When organizations decentralize data product ownership, as in the data mesh paradigm, each domain team optimizes for its immediate analytical needs, underinvesting in the cross-domain generality that enables organization-wide reuse. We formalize this as a simultaneous-move game in which N domains choose quality (q) and generality (g). Generality creates positive externalities but is privately costly. The Nash equilibrium generality gap is increasing in the number of domains and the value of cross-domain analytics. Under plausible parameter configurations, a corner solution obtains in which no reusable silver layer emerges organically, a condition we term the data mesh trap. Technical debt from narrow products grows quadratically in N. An illustrative calibration suggests non-trivial organizational welfare losses under plausible enterprise parameters. We derive within-model conditions under which centralized, federated, and hybrid governance regimes dominate, and we identify the information asymmetries and transaction costs that complicate implementation. The model provides a formal foundation for empirical research on decentralized data governance.

</details>


### [96] [Inference-Aware & Privacy-Preserving Deletion in Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00326)
*Vishal Chakraborty,Youri Kaminsky,Arnav Abhijit Dhariya,Sharad Mehrotra,Felix Naumann,Sarvesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文从推理角度重新审视数据库删除操作，识别两种隐私泄露渠道（删除后状态泄露和删除模式泄露），并以此区分逻辑删除、物理删除和语义删除，为构建具有有意义隐私保障的删除机制提供设计空间和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库系统无法提供用户期望的删除隐私保障——数据值可能从查询结果和物理存储中消失，但仍可通过依赖关系、派生数据或删除事件本身暴露的痕迹被推断出来。传统的逻辑删除或物理擦除不足以实现"有意义删除"。

Method: 采用推理中心视角，分析两个泄露通道：删除后状态的泄露（数据间依赖关系导致的信息残留）和删除模式的泄露（删除操作本身暴露的信息）。

Result: 提出语义删除的概念，区分了逻辑删除、物理删除和语义删除三个层次，并系统性地组织了删除操作的设计空间。

Conclusion: 为数据库系统中构建具有有意义隐私保障的删除机制指明了开放的研究挑战和未来方向。

Abstract: Deletion is a fundamental database operation, yet modern systems often fail to provide the privacy guarantee that users expect from it. A deleted value may disappear from query results and even from physical storage, yet remain inferable from dependencies, derived data, or traces exposed by the deletion event itself. Meaningful deletion, therefore, requires more than logical removal or physical erasure; it requires a privacy guarantee that limits what remains inferable after deletion. In this paper, we take an inference-centric view of deletion, focusing on two leakage channels: leakage from the post-deletion state and leakage from the deletion pattern itself. We use this lens to distinguish logical, physical, and semantic deletion, organize the design space of deletion operations, and highlight open research challenges for building deletion mechanisms with meaningful privacy guarantees in database systems.

</details>


### [97] [Making Array-Based Translation Practical for Modern, High-Performance Buffer Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00423)
*Xinjing Zhou,Jinming Hu,Andrew Pavlo,Michael Stonebraker*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出Calico，一个基于数组翻译的实用数据库缓冲池管理系统，可同时支持OLTP、扫描密集型分析和向量搜索等多样化工作负载，实现高达3.9倍内存性能和6.5倍超出内存性能提升，已集成到PostgreSQL中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库系统需要支持比传统OLTP更广泛的工作负载，包括扫描密集型分析和不规则高扇出图遍历访问模式的向量搜索索引。现有缓冲池设计仅能满足快速翻译、用户空间部署、巨页兼容、易集成和DBMS控制等目标的子集，无法同时满足所有需求。

Method: Calico采用基于数组的翻译机制，将逻辑翻译与OS页表解耦，使DBMS能够结合低开销翻译与巨页支持以及细粒度页面管理。为使数组翻译适用于具有大型稀疏分层页面标识符的DBMS，Calico引入了三种技术：带路径缓存的多级翻译、用于回收冷翻译内存的空洞打孔，以及利用并行性的组预取。

Result: 在扫描、OLTP风格B树访问和向量搜索等工作负载下的评估表明，Calico在内存和超出内存场景下均可达到或超越现有最优方案的性能水平。

Conclusion: 作为PostgreSQL缓冲管理器的直接替代方案，Calico与pgvector集成后，在向量搜索工作负载下实现了最高3.9倍的内存内加速和6.5倍的超出内存加速，扫描密集型查询加速最高达3倍。

Abstract: Modern buffer pools must now support a broader workload mix than classic OLTP alone. In addition to B-tree lookups, database systems increasingly serve scan-heavy analytics and vector-search indexes with irregular high-fan-out graph traversal access patterns. These workloads require a translation mechanism -- mapping logical page IDs to resident frames -- that is simultaneously fast across these diverse access patterns, deployable in user space,compatible with huge pages, easy to integrate, and still under DBMS control for eviction and I/O. Existing designs satisfy only subsets of these goals.
  This paper presents \textbf{\calico}, a practical DBMS-controlled buffer pool built around array-based translation, a decades-old-idea that was dissmissed but now viable with modern hardware.
  \calico decouples logical translation from OS page tables so that the DBMS can combine low-overhead translation with huge-page-backed frames and fine-grained page management. To make array translation practical and performant for DBMSes with large sparse hierarchical page identifiers, \calico introduces three techniques: multi-level translation with path caching, hole punching for reclaiming cold translation memory, and group prefetch to exploit parallelism.
  Our evaluation across scans, OLTP-style B-tree accesses, and vector search shows that \calico matches or outperforms the existing state-of-the-art in-memory and out-of-memory performance. We also implement \calico as a drop-in replacement for PostgreSQL's buffer manager and integrate it with \texttt{pgvector}. Across vector search, and scan-heavy workloads, \calico delivers up to 3.9$\times$ in-memory and 6.5$\times$ larger-than-memory speedup for PostgreSQL vector search, speeds up scan-heavy queries by up to 3$\times$.

</details>


### [98] [Accurate and Scalable Matrix Mechanisms via Divide and Conquer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00868)
*Guanlin He,Yingtai Xiao,Jiamu Bai,Xin Gu,Zeyu Ding,Wenpeng Yin,Daniel Kifer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuerySmasher提出了一种基于分治策略的可扩展差分隐私查询方法，通过将工作负载分割为正交子工作负载，利用现有低维矩阵机制独立最优求解后再合并，从而统一并超越 ResidualPlanner、RP+ 和 WFF 等先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵机制（如ResidualPlanner和Weighted Fourier Factorizations）虽能扩展至高维数据集并提供最优性保证，但需要一个更优的可扩展替代方案。

Method: QuerySmasher采用分治策略：1）将每个查询分割为子查询；2）将子查询重组为相互正交的子工作负载；3）利用现有低维矩阵机制独立最优回答这些小规模低维问题；4）将各解决方案缝合起来回答原始工作负载中的查询。

Result: 理论证明QuerySmasher可统一并支配RP、RP+、WFF等先前方法，在均方误差和意义下对所有工作负载均表现更优。实验验证了其可扩展性和准确性。

Conclusion: QuerySmasher不仅在理论上统一并改进了现有方法，在实践中也展现了良好的可扩展性和准确性，为差分隐私数据发布提供了一种更优的可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Matrix mechanisms are often used to provide unbiased differentially private query answers when publishing statistics or creating synthetic data. Recent work has developed matrix mechanisms, such as ResidualPlanner and Weighted Fourier Factorizations, that scale to high dimensional datasets while providing optimality guarantees for workloads such as marginals and circular product queries. They operate by adding noise to a linearly independent set of queries that can compactly represent the desired workloads.
  In this paper, we present QuerySmasher, an alternative scalable approach based on a divide-and-conquer strategy. Given a workload that can be answered from various data marginals, QuerySmasher splits each query into sub-queries and re-assembles the pieces into mutually orthogonal sub-workloads. These sub-workloads represent small, low-dimensional problems that can be independently and optimally answered by existing low-dimensional matrix mechanisms. QuerySmasher then stitches these solutions together to answer queries in the original workload.
  We show that QuerySmasher subsumes prior work, like ResidualPlanner (RP), ResidualPlanner+ (RP+), and Weighted Fourier Factorizations (WFF). We prove that it can dominate those approaches, under sum squared error, for all workloads. We also experimentally demonstrate the scalability and accuracy of QuerySmasher.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Source Known Identifiers: A Three-Tier Identity System for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00151)
*Duran Serkan Kılıç*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SKIDs三层次标识符系统，通过64位SKID、128位SKEID和加密Secure SKEID三层结构，同时满足分布式应用的存储效率、时间排序、来源验证和机密性六项需求，各层间支持确定性双向转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式标识符方案无法同时满足存储效率、时间排序、来源元数据嵌入、零查询验证、机密性和长期可寻址性六项核心需求

Method: 设计三层次身份系统：SKID(64位含时间戳+拓扑+序列号)→SKEID(128位UUID兼容+类型+时代+MAC)→Secure SKEID(AES-256加密密文)，各层支持确定性双向转换

Result: 系统同时满足全部六项标识符属性需求，各层保持互操作性，存储紧凑(8字节起)，支持数据库B-tree索引和UUID标准解析

Conclusion: SKIDs为分布式应用提供统一的标识符框架，在存储效率、时序排序、来源验证和机密性之间取得平衡，适用于跨信任边界的分布式数据库场景

Abstract: Distributed applications need identifiers that satisfy storage efficiency, chronological sortability, origin metadata embedding, zero-lookup verifiability, confidentiality for external consumers, and multi-century addressability. Based on our literature survey, no existing scheme provides all six of these identifier properties within a unified system.
  This paper introduces Source Known Identifiers (SKIDs), a three-tier identity system that projects a single entity identity across trust boundaries, addressing all six properties. The first tier, Source Known ID (SKID), is a 64-bit signed integer embedding a timestamp with a 250-millisecond precision, application topology, and a per-entity-type sequence counter. It serves as the database primary key, providing compact storage (8 bytes) and natural B-tree ordering for optimized database indexing. The second tier, Source Known Entity ID (SKEID), extends the SKID into a 128-bit Universally Unique Identifier (UUID) compatible value by adding an entity type discriminator, an epoch selector, and a BLAKE3 keyed message authentication code (MAC). SKEIDs enable zero-lookup verification of identifier origin, integrity, and entity type within trusted environments, with a big-endian byte layout that preserves chronological ordering in lexicographic UUID string comparisons. The third tier, Secure SKEID, encrypts the entire SKEID using AES-256 symmetric encryption as a single-block pseudorandom permutation, producing ciphertext indistinguishable from random bytes while remaining compatible with standard UUID data-type parsers in string representation. Deterministic bidirectional transformations connect all three tiers.

</details>


### [100] [From Skew to Symmetry: Node-Interconnect Multi-Path Balancing with Execution-time Planning for Modern GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00317)
*Jinghan Yao,Kaushik Kandadi,Bharath Ramesh,Hari Subramoni,Dhabaleswar K. Panda*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NIMBLE是一种GPU集群通信优化系统，通过动态流量调度和多路径负载均衡解决GPU集群中流量倾斜导致的链路利用率不足问题，在H100集群上最高可实现5.2倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU集群尽管具备高聚合带宽，但流量倾斜常导致部分链路过载而其他链路闲置，现有NCCL和MPI等框架依赖静态路由或简单哈希条带化，无法适应运行时流量分布变化，造成拥塞、延迟突增和可扩展性差。

Method: NIMBLE将问题建模为容量归一化最小拥塞优化问题，采用乘法权重算法高效求解；结合CUDA感知的GPU内核RDMA流水线技术，通过中间GPU和rail匹配的NIC路由流量；系统采用端点驱动架构，可透明集成到现有通信库，无需应用修改，同时保序、确定性和低开销。

Result: 在H100-SXM4节点（NVLink全互联+四NDR400 rail）上：节点内带宽提升2.3倍，节点间吞吐提升3.8倍；偏斜All-to-Allv工作负载下优于NCCL/MPI达5.2倍；LLM MoE端到端任务提升1.35倍；均衡流量下性能持平基线。

Conclusion: NIMBLE通过动态多路径流量调度有效解决了GPU集群通信中的流量倾斜问题，在各种工作负载下均展现出显著性能优势，同时保持了与现有系统的兼容性和确定性语义。

Abstract: Modern GPU-based high-performance computing clusters offer unprecedented communication bandwidth through heterogeneous intra-node interconnects and inter-node networks. However, despite this high aggregate bandwidth, many real-world communication patterns fail to fully utilize the available hardware. Traffic skew often leads to situations where a small subset of links becomes oversaturated while others remain underutilized, resulting in congestion, latency spikes, and poor scalability. Existing communication frameworks such as NCCL and MPI with UCX typically rely on static fastest-path routing or hashing-based multi-rail striping, which leaves significant bandwidth unused when runtime traffic deviates from expected distributions. To address these limitations, we propose NIMBLE (Node-Interconnect Multi-path Balancing with Execution-time orchestration), a runtime communication orchestration system that dynamically redistributes traffic to balance link utilization across all available intra-node and inter-node paths. NIMBLE formulates this as a capacity-normalized minimum-congestion optimization problem and solves it efficiently using a multiplicative-weights algorithm. It further employs CUDA-aware GPU kernel-based RDMA pipelining to route traffic through intermediate GPUs and rail-matched NICs. The system is endpoint-driven, integrates transparently with existing communication libraries without requiring application changes, and preserves ordering, determinism, and low overhead. On H100-SXM4 nodes with fully connected NVLink and four NDR400 rails, NIMBLE achieves up to 2.3x higher intra-node bandwidth and 3.8x higher inter-node throughput compared to single-path baselines. It outperforms NCCL and MPI by up to 5.2x on skewed All-to-Allv workloads and 1.35x on end-to-end LLM MoE workloads, while matching baseline performance under balanced traffic.

</details>


### [101] [TENT: A Declarative Slice Spraying Engine for Performant and Resilient Data Movement in Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00368)
*Feng Ren,Ruoyu Qin,Teng Ma,Shangming Cai,Zheng Liu,Chao Lei,Dejiang Zhu,Ke Yang,Zheming Li,Jialei Cui,Weixiao Huang,Yikai Zhao,Yineng Zhang,Hao Wu,Xiang Gao,Yuhao Fu,Jinlei Jiang,Yongwei Wu,Mingxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TENT是一个数据移动引擎，通过将传输意图与物理执行解耦，统一异构互连为动态资源池，实现基于实时链路质量的流量切片与动态路由，消除队头阻塞，支持亚50ms自愈，显著提升LLM推理和RL训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（如Mooncake TE）采用命令式、静态绑定的路径选择机制，存在三大核心问题：状态无关的条带化策略忽视拥塞信号、多轨道带宽因队头阻塞而浪费、故障恢复依赖手动干预。这些问题在千卡规模LLM服务场景下严重制约系统效率和稳定性。

Method: TENT提出传输意图与物理执行解耦的架构：应用层仅声明传输意图，引擎内部将大流分解为细粒度切片，基于实时链路质量在异构互连（多轨道RDMA、NVLink、Ascend UB等）间动态"喷洒"传输，同时内置透明自愈机制实现故障自动绕路。

Result: 在H800 HGX集群评估中，TENT全面超越Mooncake TE、NIXL、UCCL等SOTA基线。LLM推理场景下，结合SGLang HiCache实现1.36倍吞吐量和26%的P90 TTFT降低；RL训练场景下，Moonshot Checkpoint Engine参数更新加速20-26%。

Conclusion: TENT通过动态遥测驱动的编排策略，成功解决了异构互连场景下的通信效率与可靠性难题，已在多个工业站点完成生产部署验证，为LLM Serving数据平面提供了高效、弹性、自动化的大规模传输解决方案。

Abstract: Modern GPU clusters are built upon a complex hierarchy of heterogeneous interconnects, ranging from multi-rail RDMA to proprietary fabrics such as Multi-Node NVLink and Ascend UB. Orchestrating these diverse links effectively remains a critical challenge in disaggregated LLM serving. Operating Mooncake TE on thousands of GPUs exposed a critical limitation shared by existing frameworks: imperative, statically bound path selection. This rigidity forces engines to rely on state-blind striping that ignores congestion signals, creating communication silos, wasting multi-rail bandwidth due to head-of-line blocking, and leading to operational fragility where routine faults require manual intervention. We present TENT, a data-movement engine that decouples transfer intent from physical execution. Instead of locking workloads to fixed backends, TENT unifies heterogeneous interconnects into a single dynamic resource pool. Applications simply declare transfer intents, while TENT dynamically decomposes elephant flows into fine-grained slices and "sprays" them across links based on instantaneous link quality. This telemetry-driven orchestration eliminates head-of-line blocking and enables transparent, sub-50 ms self-healing by rerouting slices around failures without application logic. TENT serves as the production data plane for LLM inference and RL pipelines at multiple industrial sites. Our evaluation on H800 HGX clusters shows that TENT outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including Mooncake TE, NIXL, and UCCL. In LLM inference with SGLang HiCache, TENT achieves up to 1.36x higher throughput and 26% lower P90 TTFT than Mooncake TE. In RL pipelines, TENT accelerates parameter updates in Moonshot Checkpoint Engine by 20-26%.

</details>


### [102] [Reclaiming Idle CPU Cycles on Kubernetes: Sparse-Domain Multiplexing for Concurrent MPI-CFD Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00377)
*Tianfang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在共享Kubernetes集群上运行MPI并行模拟时，传统CPU调度导致大量周期在同步屏障处闲置。本文提出一个多路复用框架，通过在相同集群上共置多个模拟来回收这些空闲容量，实现1.77x-3.74x吞吐量提升，并首次将In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling应用于运行中的MPI进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享Kubernetes集群上运行MPI并行模拟时，传统的CPU调度方式导致大量已配置的CPU周期在同步屏障处处于空闲状态，造成严重的资源浪费。

Method: 1. 使用PMPI（过程间MPI）进行工作周期分析，量化每个进程的空闲比例；2. 基于比例的CPU分配允许第二个模拟并发执行；3. Pareto分析找到最优并发数量；4. 构建包含单个拟合参数的解析模型预测收益；5. 开发动态控制器自动化整个流程，包括profiling、In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling（KEP-1287）、打包和公平性监控。

Result: 静态配置下单模拟吞吐量提升1.77x，Pareto扫描到N=5个并发模拟时达3.74x，N=3为效率-成本最佳点；解析模型预测精度在±4%以内；动态控制器在无需人工干预或Pod重启的情况下，为四个并发模拟实现3.25x吞吐量提升。AWS集群上OpenFOAM CFD测试验证了该方法在同心分区和Scotch图分区下均有效。

Conclusion: 该框架首次成功将In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling应用于运行中的MPI进程，通过回收同步屏障处的空闲CPU周期，可显著提升共享Kubernetes集群上MPI模拟的吞吐量，且解析模型能够准确预测性能收益。

Abstract: When MPI-parallel simulations run on shared Kubernetes clusters, conventional CPU scheduling leaves the vast majority of provisioned cycles idle at synchronization barriers. This paper presents a multiplexing framework that reclaims this idle capacity by co-locating multiple simulations on the same cluster. PMPI-based duty-cycle profiling quantifies the per-rank idle fraction; proportional CPU allocation then allows a second simulation to execute concurrently with minimal overhead, yielding 1.77x throughput. A Pareto sweep to N=5 concurrent simulations shows throughput scaling to 3.74x, with a knee at N=3 offering the best efficiency-cost trade-off. An analytical model with a single fitted parameter predicts these gains within +/-4%. A dynamic controller automates the full pipeline, from profiling through In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling (KEP-1287) to packing and fairness monitoring, achieving 3.25x throughput for four simulations without manual intervention or pod restarts. To our knowledge, this is the first CPU application of In-Place Pod Vertical Scaling to running MPI processes. Experiments on an AWS cluster with OpenFOAM CFD confirm that the results hold under both concentric and standard graph-based (Scotch) mesh partitioning.

</details>


### [103] [MPI-Q: A Message Communication Library for Large-Scale Classical-Quantum Heterogeneous Hybrid Distributed Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00600)
*Feng Wang,Junchao Wang,Zeyuan Wang,Lei Li,Yangyang Fei,Jinyang Yao,Xuyan Qi,Fudong Liu,Yifan Hou,Shibo Liang,Zheng Shan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对经典-量子异构分布式系统中现有通信机制（如MPI、NCCL）的不足，提出MPI-Q消息传递库，通过异构混合通信域、 lightweight通信路径和异构混合同步机制三大设计，实现对量子子系统的扩展支持，在24量子节点上实现18.76倍加速，验证了大规模异构混合分布式计算的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典-量子系统异构性（数据特征、执行范式、同步机制等差异）导致现有分布式通信机制（如MPI、NCCL）无法有效支持，限制了混合应用的操作协同和编程效率。

Method: 提出针对大规模经典-量子异构分布式计算的消息传递库MPI-Q，包含三个核心机制：1）定义异构混合通信域，实现经典和量子进程的统一管理；2）采用lightweight通信路径，允许经典控制节点直接将设备就绪的波形数据发送给量子MonitorProcess，避免不必要的 relay 阶段；3）建立异构混合同步机制，解决多节点量子操作的时序控制问题。

Result: 在分布式GHZ态制备实验中，该模型展现出近线性可扩展性，在24个量子节点上实现最大18.76倍加速。

Conclusion: MPI-Q能有效支持大规模异构混合分布式计算应用，填补了该领域的技术空白，同时保持了传统MPI编程模型的兼容性。

Abstract: The classical-quantum system heterogeneity (different data characteristics, execution paradigms and synchronization mechanism etc.) renders existing distributed communication mechanisms (e.g. MPI, NCCL etc.) inadequate. This bottleneck severely impairs operational synergy and programming efficiency. Thus, the performance of hybrid applications on classical-quantum heterogeneous infrastructures is directly limited.
  To address these challenges, this paper proposes a message-passing library tailored for large-scale classical-quantum heterogeneous distributed computing, referred to as MPI-Q. The design centers on three mechanisms. First, it defines a heterogeneous hybrid communication domain that achieves unified management of classical and quantum processes in heterogeneous hybrid systems. Second, it uses a lightweight communication path that allows classical control nodes to send device-ready waveform data directly to quantum MonitorProcesses, avoiding unnecessary relay stages. Third, it establishes a heterogeneous hybrid synchronization mechanism to tackle the problem of timing control for multi-node quantum operations. While retaining the traditional MPI programming model, MPI-Q achieves extension toward quantum subsystems. Experiments on distributed GHZ state preparation demonstrate that this model exhibits near-linear scalability, achieving a maximum speedup of 18.76 times on 24 quantum nodes. This proves that the library can effectively support large-scale heterogeneous hybrid distributed computing applications, filling the technical gap in this field.

</details>


### [104] [Is RISC-V Ready for Machine Learning? Portable Gaussian Processes Using Asynchronous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00736)
*Alexander Strack,Patrick Diehl,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于HPX异步运行时系统的GPRat高斯过程计算库在x86-64 (Zen 2)、ARM (A64FX) 和RISC-V (SG2042) 三种硬件架构上的性能表现。研究表明ARM芯片凭借更好的并行扩展性在多核场景下超越x86-64，而RISC-V芯片因向量化和内存子系统限制表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程计算在大规模机器学习应用中面临计算密集型挑战，需要在多样化硬件平台上实现高效可扩展的部署，现有方案缺乏跨架构的便携性和并行效率评估。

Method: 使用HPX异步多任务运行时系统扩展GPRat库，开展节点级强扩展性测试和问题规模扩展性测试，在三种代表性芯片上进行单核性能、并行扩展性和架构效率的综合评估。

Result: x86-64 Zen 2单核性能比ARM A64FX高58%，但ARM的48核配置凭借更优并行扩展性在满节点时比64核Zen 2快9%。RISC-V SG2042单核性能落后达14倍，大规模并行工作负载减速达25倍。问题规模扩展测试中，ARM与x86-64性能差距在25%以内。

Conclusion: ARM处理器在并行计算场景中竞争力日益增强，RISC-V平台需重点改进宽寄存器向量化支持和内存子系统性能以提升竞争力。

Abstract: Gaussian processes are widely used in machine learning domains but remain computationally demanding, limiting their efficient scalability across diverse hardware platforms. The GPRat library targets these challenges with the help of the asynchronous many-task runtime system HPX. In this work, we extend GPRat to enable portability across multiple hardware architectures and evaluate its performance on representative x86-64, ARM, and RISC-V chips. We conduct node-level strong-scaling and problem-size-scaling benchmarks for Gaussian Process prediction and hyperparameter optimization to assess single-core performance, parallel scalability, and architectural efficiency.
  Our results show that while the x86-64 Zen 2 chip achieves a 58% single-core performance advantage over the ARM-based Fujitsu A64FX, superior parallel scaling allows the 48-core ARM chip to outperform the 64-core Zen 2 by 9% at full node utilization. The evaluated SOPHON SG2042 RISC-V chip exhibits substantially lower performance and weaker scalability, with single-core performance lagging by up to a factor of 14 and large-scale parallel workloads showing slowdowns of up to a factor of 25. For problem-size scaling, ARM and x86-64 systems demonstrate comparable performance within 25%. These findings highlight the growing competitiveness of ARM-based processors and emphasize the importance of wide-register vectorization support and memory subsystem improvements for upcoming RISC-V platforms.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>
